Gym-Anything：将任何软件变成代理环境Gym-Anything: Turn any Software into an Agent Environment

普兰贾尔·阿加瓦尔Pranjal Aggarwal卡耐基梅隆大学CMU

格雷厄姆·纽比格 肖恩·维尔克Graham Neubig Sean Welleck卡耐基梅隆大学CMU CMU

https://cmu-l3.github.io/gym-anything

摘要Abstract

计算机使用代理有望协助广泛的数字经济活动。然而，当前的研究主要集中在经济价值有限的有限软件集上的短期任务，例如基本的电子商务和操作系统配置任务。一个关键原因是，为复杂软件创建环境需要大量时间和人力，因此无法扩展。为了解决这个问题，我们引入Computer-use agents hold the promise of assisting in a wide range of digital economic activities. However, current research has largely focused on short-horizon tasks over a limited set of software with limited economic value, such as basic e- commerce and OS-configuration tasks. A key reason is that creating environments for complex software requires significant time and human effort, and therefore does not scale. To address this, we introduce健身房-任何事情Gym-Anything，一个框架, a framework for将任何软件转换为交互式计算机使用环境converting any software into an interactive computer-use environment。我们将环境创建本身构建为多代理任务：编码代理编写设置脚本、下载真实数据并配置软件，同时生成正确设置的证据。然后，独立审计代理根据质量检查表验证环境设置的证据。我们使用基于美国 GDP 数据的具有经济价值的职业分类法，将此管道应用于 200 个具有广泛职业覆盖范围的软件应用程序。结果是. We frame environment creation itself as a multi-agent task: a coding agent writes setup scripts, downloads real-world data, and configures the software, while producing evidence of correct setup. An independent audit agent then verifies evidence for the environment setup against a quality checklist. Using a taxonomy of economically valuable occupations grounded in U.S. GDP data, we apply this pipeline to 200 software applications with broad occupational coverage. The result isCUA-World，超过 10K 个长期任务的集合，涵盖从医学科学和天文学到工程和企业系统的领域，每个任务都配置有真实的数据以及训练和测试分割。, a collection of over 10K long-horizon tasks spanning domains from medical science and astronomy to engineering and enterprise systems, each configured with realistic data along with train and test splits.CUA-World还包括also includesCUA-World-Long，一个具有挑战性的长期基准，其任务通常需要超过 500 个步骤，远远超过现有基准。将训练分割中的成功轨迹提炼为 2B 视觉语言模型，其性能优于模型 2-Long, a challenging long-horizon benchmark with tasks often requiring over 500 steps, far exceeding existing benchmarks. Distilling successful trajectories from the training split into a 2B vision-language model outperforms models 2×它的大小。我们还在测试时应用相同的审核原则：单独的 VLM 审查已完成的轨迹并提供剩余轨迹的反馈，从而改进 Gemini-3-Flashits size. We also apply the same auditing principle at test time: a separate VLM reviews completed trajectories and provides feedback on what remains, improving Gemini-3-Flash onCUA-World-从 11.5% 到 14.0% 长。我们发布所有代码、基础设施和基准数据，以促进未来对实际计算机使用代理的研究。-Long from 11.5% to 14.0%. We release all code, infrastructure, and benchmark data to facilitate future research in realistic computer-use agents.

图1：Figure 1:内置Built with Gym-Anything , CUA-World 涵盖所有主要职业群体和行业，covers all major occupation groups and industries,跨越 200 个软件应用程序的超过 10K+ 长期任务和环境，极大地扩展了计算机使用代理评估和培训的范围。spanning over 10K+ long-horizon tasks and environments across 200 software applications, dramatically expanding the scope of computer-use agent evaluation and training.

预印本。Preprint.

1 简介1 Introduction

计算机使用代理（CUA，也称为图形用户界面代理）有望实现数字密集型职业的自动化和协助，这些职业总共代表了数万亿美元的 GDP [46, 45]。然而，这些代理人是否能够处理真正的专业工作仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。现实世界的工作流程是Computer-use agents (CUAs, also known as graphical user interface agents) hold the promise of automating and assisting in digitally intensive occupations, which collectively represent trillions of dollars in GDP [46, 45]. Yet whether these agents can handle real professional work remains an open question. Real-world workflows are长远long-horizon并发生在and take place in异构环境heterogeneous environments，通常需要跨配置有特定领域数据的不同软件执行数百个步骤。例如，医学成像数据集的端到端分析需要使用带注释的临床 CT 扫描设置的放射学工具，而跨企业资源规划 (ERP) 系统协调财务记录则需要填充交易历史记录和供应商帐户的软件。现有的基准测试对这些功能的了解很少，因为它们主要测试代理在短期任务上的表现，例如更改桌面壁纸或填写网络表单，以及一组狭窄的消费级应用程序[53,62,23,7,37,11]，这些应用程序仅代表经济的一小部分[51]。, often requiring hundreds of steps across diverse software configured with domain-specific data. For instance, end-to-end analysis of a medical imaging dataset requires a radiology tool set up with annotated clinical CT scans, while reconciling financial records across an enterprise resource planning (ERP) system requires the software populated with transaction histories and vendor accounts. Existing benchmarks shed little light on these capabilities, as they largely test agents on short-horizon tasks such as changing a desktop wallpaper or filling a web form, over a narrow set of consumer-grade applications [53, 62, 23, 7, 37, 11] that only represent a small slice of the economy [51].

这种差距有两个后果。首先，This gap has two consequences. First,评价不真实evaluation is unfaithful：当前基准的高分几乎无法揭示代理人操作驱动实际经济活动的软件的能力。第二，: high scores on current benchmarks reveal little about an agent’s ability to operate the software that drives real economic activity. Second,训练信号有限training signal is limited：少数应用程序的短期任务可能无法产生训练代理进行实际工作所需的多样化的长期轨迹。根本原因是创建现实环境的成本过高：每个软件都需要安装、使用适合领域的数据进行配置、任务设计和验证，每个应用程序通常需要数周的专家努力[53, 56]。我们要解决的核心问题是：: short-horizon tasks over few applications may not produce the diverse, long-horizon trajectories needed to train agents for real-world work. The root cause is that creating realistic environments is prohibitively expensive: each software requires installation, configuration with domain-appropriate data, task design, and verification, often demanding weeks of expert effort per application [53, 56]. The core question we aim to address is:我们如何才能在更接近现实工作的环境中扩展计算机使用环境以进行培训和基准测试？how can we scale computeruse environments for training and benchmarking agents in settings closer to real-world work?为了解决这个问题，我们引入To address this, we introduceGym-Anything，一个可扩展的框架，用于跨数百个具有经济价值的软件应用程序自动构建现实环境。其核心是，, a scalable framework for automatically constructing realistic environments across hundreds of economically valuable software applications. At its core,Gym-Anything允许转弯allows turning将任何软件集成到可交互的环境中。any software into an interactable environment.我们根据美国数字 GDP 数据进行软件选择，根据高经济影响力和跨战略和 STEM 领域、不同职业和行业的广泛覆盖范围来选择软件（图 2 (i)）。We ground the software selection on U.S. digital GDP data, selecting software based on high economic impact and broad coverage across strategic and STEM domains, different occupations, and industries (Figure 2 (i)).

背后的关键思想The key idea behind theGym-Anything该框架与其他领域[64, 60]中探索的代理驱动环境构建类似，是framework, similar to agent-driven environment construction explored in other domains [64, 60], is that创建计算机使用代理环境本身就是一项编码和计算机使用代理任务。creating computer-use agent environments is itself a coding and computer-use agent task.设置软件需要编写安装、配置和数据处理脚本，这些都是编码任务。验证环境是否正确启动并达到预期状态需要启动它、截图并检查屏幕，这些都是计算机使用代理任务。然而，将计算机使用环境构建扩展到数百种类型的软件需要处理大量的异构性，包括不同的操作系统、数据库和网络配置。为了解决这个问题，我们开发了Setting up software requires writing installation, configuration, and data-processing scripts, which are coding tasks. Verifying that the environment starts correctly and reaches the expected state requires launching it, taking screenshots, and checking the screen, which are computer-use agent tasks. However, scaling computer-use environment construction to hundreds of types of software requires handling substantial heterogeneity, including different operating systems, databases, and network configurations. To address this, we develop theGym-Anything库（§2.3），它将每个环境简化为标准化规范：一小组安装脚本和一个配置文件。反过来，该库使人工智能代理能够通过仅编写特定于软件的脚本而不是处理低级基础设施来创建环境。library (§2.3), which reduces every environment to a standardized specification: a small set of setup scripts and a configuration file. In turn, the library enables an AI agent to create environments by writing only software-specific scripts rather than dealing with low-level infrastructure.

然而，如果没有外部验证，当前提示在此框架中创建环境的代理经常会产生不正确的环境设置。共同点是，代理对其所做的事情的声明并不可靠，但环境的实际状态是可靠的。例如，无论代理声称什么，正在运行的软件的屏幕截图都会显示环境是否正常工作或卡在崩溃屏幕上。我们通过以下方式利用这一观察结果：However, without external verification, current agents prompted to create environments in this framework frequently produce incorrect environment setups. The common thread is that the agent’s claims about what it has done are not reliable, but the actual state of the environment is. For instance, a screenshot of the running software reveals whether the environment is working or stuck on a crash screen, regardless of what the agent claims. We exploit this observation through a创建-审核循环creation-audit loop（§3；图 2 (ii)），其中创建代理构建环境并通过屏幕截图、执行日志和数据输出生成正确设置的证据，然后独立审计代理根据质量检查表验证证据并报告问题。此外，创建代理还构建了它在尝试中发现的环境创建策略的共享内存，从而允许代理随着时间的推移而改进。(§3; Figure 2 (ii)), in which a creation agent builds an environment and produces evidence of a correct setup through screenshots, execution logs, and data outputs, then an independent audit agent verifies the evidence against a quality checklist and reports issues. In addition, the creation agent builds a shared memory of environment creation strategies that it discovers across attempts, allowing the agent to improve over time.

接下来，我们采用一个Next, we adopt a提出并扩大propose-and-amplify用于在软件环境中大规模生成实际任务的策略（图 2 (iii)；§4）。在这种模式中，昂贵的代理模型为每个软件提出少量种子任务并运行这些任务，然后更便宜的非代理语言模型使用种子实现作为上下文示例将这些种子放大到更大的集合。为了评估生成的任务，我们使用基于清单的 VLM 验证器，将每个任务分解为部分得分的加权子任务（第 4.1 节）。为了构建检查表，我们利用环境设置脚本中嵌入的数据（例如，下载的医疗数据集中的正确肿瘤位置）。重要的是，代理在解决任务时无法访问此信息，从而使其成为一种形式strategy (Figure 2 (iii); §4) for generating realistic tasks at scale within the software environments. In this pattern, an expensive agentic model proposes a small number of seed tasks per software and runs the tasks, and then a cheaper non-agentic language model amplifies these seeds into a larger set using the seed implementations as in-context examples. To evaluate the resulting tasks, we use a checklist-based VLM verifier that breaks each task into weighted subtasks for partial credit (§4.1). To construct the checklists, we leverage data that is embedded in the environment’s setup scripts (e.g., the correct tumor location from a downloaded medical dataset). Importantly, agents do not have access to this information when solving a task, thereby making it a form of特权信息privileged information验证者可以利用它来检查代理的输出。that the verifier can leverage in order to check the agent’s outputs.

The creation agent writes its learnings into a memory, allowing it to improve over time.第三阶段：Phase 3:任务按比例缩放Tasks are scaled with a提出并扩大propose-and-amplify模式，其中昂贵的代理模型创建高质量的种子任务（例如，每个软件 5 个），然后更便宜的语言模型生成更多任务（例如，75 个）pattern, in which an expensive agentic model creates high-quality seed tasks (e.g., 5 per software), then a cheaper language model generates more tasks (e.g., 75×）使用种子作为上下文示例。) using the seeds as in-context examples.第四阶段：Phase 4:代理商的评估依据Agents are evaluated onCUA-World使用基于清单的 VLM 验证程序，其中包含特权信息和细粒度的评分标准。using a checklist-based VLM verifier with privileged information and fine-grained rubric scores.

我们使用We useGym-Anything建造to constructCUA-World，跨越 200 个软件应用程序的 10,000 多个任务的集合。, a collection of over 10,000 tasks across 200 software applications.CUA-World涵盖医学、天文学、工程、金融、企业系统和教育平台等领域。它包括三个操作系统上的任务，并分为训练和测试部分（表 2）。为了证明训练分割的实用性，我们将轨迹从强大的教师模型提炼为 2B 视觉语言模型，并发现性能随着训练集中软件和环境的数量而变化。训练后的模型还可以推广到训练期间未见过的软件，并且性能优于模型 2spans domains such as medical science, astronomy, engineering, finance, enterprise systems, and educational platforms. It includes tasks on three operating systems, and is separated into train and test splits (Table 2). To demonstrate the utility of the training split, we distill trajectories from a strong teacher model into a 2B vision-language model and find that performance scales with the number of software and environments in the training set. The trained model also generalizes to software not seen during training, and outperforms models 2×它的大小。its size.

鉴于其广度和现实性Given the breadth and realism ofCUA-World的软件和任务，我们进一步构建了一个具有挑战性的长期基准，’s software and tasks, we further construct a challenging long-horizon benchmark,CUA-World- 长，每个软件由一项任务组成，任务通常需要数百个步骤。每项任务都设计得切合实际，并针对现有模型的常见故障模式。即使是最强的前沿模型，通过率也只有 27.5%-Long, consisting of one task per software, with tasks often requiring hundreds of steps. Each task is designed to be realistic and to target common failure modes of existing models. Even the strongest frontier model achieves only 27.5% pass rate onCUA-World- 长，突出长期任务的难度。一种常见的失败模式是代理经常提前停止，声称任务已完成，但实际上并未完成。为了解决这个问题，我们应用了类似的测试时审核原则（第 6.2 节），其中独立模型在完成后审查代理的轨迹，并提供剩余内容的反馈，从而将 Gemini-3-Flash 的通过率从 11.5% 提高到 14.0%。尽管测试时审核有帮助，- Long, highlighting the difficulty of long-horizon tasks. One common failure mode is that agents often stop early, claiming the task is complete when it is not. To address this, we apply a similar Test-Time Auditing principle (§6.2), where an independent model reviews the agent’s trajectory upon completion and provides feedback on what remains, improving pass rate from 11.5% to 14.0% for Gemini-3-Flash. Although Test-Time Auditing helps,CUA-World-Long 问题在很大程度上仍未得到解决，为前沿计算机使用代理执行实际任务提供了一个新的具有挑战性的基准。-Long remains largely unsolved, offering a new challenging benchmark for frontier computer-use agents on realistic tasks.

总而言之，我们贡献（1）In summary, we contribute (1)Gym-Anything，一个模块化框架和多代理管道，用于将任何软件转换为交互式计算机使用环境； (2), a modular framework and multi-agent pipeline for converting any software into an interactive computer-use environment; (2)CUA-World，涵盖 10,000 个任务的集合，涵盖基于 GDP 的 200 个软件应用程序选择，具有基于清单的 VLM 验证、训练/测试分割和具有挑战性的长期分割（, a collection of 10,000 tasks across a GDP-grounded selection of 200 software applications with checklist-based VLM verification, train/test splits, and a challenging long-horizon split (CUA-World-长）需要数百步； (3) 训练和测试时间缩放结果，包括蒸馏为优于模型 2 的 2B 模型-Long) requiring hundreds of steps; (3) training and test-time scaling results, including distillation to a 2B model that outperforms models 2×它的大小，以及一个测试时审计代理，可以提高长期性能its size, and a test-time audit agent that improves long-horizon performance onCUA-World-长； (4) 完整发布所有代码、基础设施和基准数据。-Long; and (4) a full release of all code, infrastructure, and benchmark data.

2 方法论2 Methodology

在本节中，我们将介绍问题设置、基于 GDP 的软件选择过程以及使大规模环境构建成为可能的库抽象。在第 3 节中，我们进一步讨论了扩展软件数量的多代理策略，在第 4 节中，讨论了如何进一步扩展相关软件的任务和环境。In this section, we introduce the problem setup, the GDP-grounded software selection procedure, and the library abstraction that makes large-scale environment construction possible. In Section 3, we further discuss our multi-agent strategy to scale the number of software, and in Section 4, discuss how to further scale tasks and environments for the relevant software.

2.1 问题设置2.1 Problem Setup

环境Environment我们指的是一个环境We refer to an environmentE作为一个或多个交互式软件应用程序，具有指定的文件系统初始状态和在操作系统内运行的进程。代理通过键盘和鼠标操作与环境交互。as one or more interactive software applications with a specified initial state of the filesystem and processes running inside an operating system. The agent interacts with the environment through keyboard and mouse actions.

任务。Task.一个任务A taskT=（= (Es 0 , p, V）由一个环境组成) consists of an environmentE具有初始状态with initial states0、自然语言指令0, a natural-language instructionp，以及验证函数, and a verification functionV将智能体的轨迹映射到分数。that maps the agent’s trajectory to a score.

互动。Interaction.每一步At each stept，代理收到观察, the agent receives an observationot（例如，屏幕截图）并执行鼠标/键盘操作(e.g., a screen capture) and executes mouse/keyboard actionsat，之后环境返回一个新的观察结果, after which the environment returns a new observationot+1。情节通过将环境重置为+1. An episode proceeds by resetting the environment tos0，让代理交互最多0, letting the agent interact for up toT步数，最终得分由下式决定：steps, and the final score is determined byV .

2.2 基于 GDP 的软件选择2.2 GDP-Grounded Software Selection

确定 CUA 训练数据集和基准测试中包含哪些软件是一项重要的设计决策。我们使用一个简单的原则：Determining which software to include in a CUA training dataset and benchmark is an important design decision. We use a simple principle:优先考虑能够推动更多经济活动的软件。prioritize software that drives more economic activity.与之前的基准不同，我们的选择基于公开的美国数字经济数据（图 3）。简而言之，我们估算每个职业的 GDP，发现每个职业使用的软件，将 GDP 归因于单个软件，筛选可沙箱候选者，应用分层选择，并根据我们的计算预算选择 200 个软件应用程序。我们将在下面详细介绍每个步骤。Unlike prior benchmarks, we ground our selection in publicly available U.S. digital economy data (Figure 3). In a nutshell, we estimate GDP per occupation, discover software used by each occupation, attribute GDP to individual software, filter to sandboxable candidates, apply tiered selection, and based on our compute budget select 200 software applications. We detail each step below.

估算每个职业的 GDP。Estimating GDP per occupation.O*NET 美国经济数据包括The O*NET data on the U.S. economy comprises∼900 个标准化职业，每个职业都有就业人数和平均工资的公开数据。[1]对于900 standardized occupations, each with publicly available data on employment counts and average wages.[1] For

每个职业，我们计算工资单（就业each occupation, we compute a wage bill (employment×平均工资）并使用国民账户数据将其缩放至 GDP 总量 [45]，得出每个职业的 GDP 估计值，其总和等于美国全部 GDP（附录 A）。mean wage) and scale it to total GDP using national accounts data [45], yielding a GDP estimate per occupation that sums to the full U.S. GDP (Appendix A).

发现每个职业的软件。Discovering software per occupation.接下来，我们需要知道每个职业实际使用什么软件。我们使用具有网络搜索访问权限的法学硕士来发现相关的软件类别，并枚举每个职业的每个类别的软件，生成一个目录Next, we need to know what software each occupation actually uses. We use an LLM with web-search access to discover relevant software categories and enumerate software per category for each occupation, producing a catalog of∼16,600 个软件应用程序16,600 software applications across∼1,400 个类别。通过删除重复类别、验证软件类别分配以及通过基于网络的验证删除幻觉条目来清理目录。1,400 categories. The catalog is cleaned by deduplicating categories, validating software-category assignments, and removing hallucinated entries via web-grounded verification.

将 GDP 归因于软件。Attributing GDP to software.并非所有职业的产出都涉及计算机，也并非所有计算机工作都使用相同的软件。我们将每个职业的 GDP 分解为软件级别的估算：Not all of an occupation’s output involves computers, and not all computer work uses the same software. We decompose each occupation’s GDP into a software-level estimate:

在这里，Here,p计算机是涉及计算机的职业工作的一部分（可从职业调查中获得），computer is the fraction of the occupation’s work that involves computers (available from occupational surveys),s类别是归因于软件类别的计算机工作的份额（例如会计师的“电子表格”），以及category is the share of that computer work attributed to a software category (e.g., “spreadsheets” for an accountant), ands软件是软件在其类别中的份额（例如，Excel 在电子表格中的份额）。份额因素software is the software’s share within its category (e.g., Excel’s share of spreadsheets). 过滤到可沙箱软件。Filtering to sandboxable software.并非所有经济上重要的软件都可以沙箱到交互式环境中，因为它们可能需要付费许可证、组织凭证或专用硬件。如果每个软件应用程序满足以下限制，我们将其分类为沙箱就绪：（a）可自托管，即不需要在线帐户即可使用，（b）免费层，即可以免费使用而不受许可证限制，（c）具有GUI，并且（d）不需要无法模拟的硬件（例如数控机床）。我们选择Not all economically important software can be sandboxed into an interactive environment, since they may require paid licenses, organizational credentials, or specialized hardware. We classify each software application as sandbox-ready if it satisfies the following constraints: (a) self-hostable, i.e., does not require an online account to use, (b) free-tier, i.e., can be used free of charge without license restrictions, (c) has a GUI, and (d) does not require hardware that cannot be simulated (e.g., CNC machines). We select∼3,400 个3,400 of∼16,600 满足这些限制。此外，当软件不可沙箱化时，我们会替换同一软件类别中最接近的沙箱替代品，旨在保留经济信号。16,600 that satisfy these constraints. Further, when a software is not sandboxable, we substitute the closest sandboxable alternative from the same software category, aiming to preserve the economic signal.

分层选择。Tiered selection.从过滤后的目录中，我们选择了五个层次的 200 个软件应用程序，平衡了经济重要性与多样性：(1) 总体 GDP 最高的软件，(2a/2b) 具有战略意义的重要领域（医疗保健、教育、防护服务、交通）和 STEM 领域（建筑/工程、计算机/数学、生命/物理/社会科学），(3) 循环浏览所有 22 个 SOC 主要组以选择From the filtered catalog, we select 200 software applications across five tiers that balance economic importance with diversity: (1) highest-GDP software overall, (2a/2b) strategically important domains (Healthcare, Education, Protective Services, Transportation) and STEM domains (Architecture/Engineering, Computer/Math, Life/Physical/Social Science), (3) cycling through all 22 SOC major groups to select∼每组 5 个，确保每个职业组都有代表性，(4) 特定职业或尚未涵盖的领域独有的软件，以及 (5) 未覆盖类别的软件，按 GDP 排名。尽管管道是完全自动化且可扩展的，但我们根据计算预算为 200 个软件应用程序构建了环境（附录 A）。5 per group, ensuring every occupation group has representation, (4) software unique to specific occupations or domains not yet covered, and (5) software from uncovered categories, ranked by GDP. We build environments for 200 software applications based on our compute budget, although the pipeline is fully automated and extensible (Appendix A).

2.3 Gym-Anything 库2.3 The Gym-Anything Library

跨数百种不同的软件应用程序构建计算机使用环境需要一个统一的框架，该框架可以跨操作系统、应用程序类型和计算后端工作，而无需针对每个环境进行工程设计。以前的工作主要是通过与实际操作系统交互来手动构建计算机使用环境，然后拍摄虚拟机快照以供以后重用[53]。但是，快照无法进行检查、版本控制或跨任务部分重用，并且修改任何内容都需要重复手动设置，因此限制了模块化、可重复性和可扩展性。为了应对这些挑战，我们构建了Constructing computer-use environments across hundreds of diverse software applications requires a unified framework that works across operating systems, application types, and compute backends without per-environment engineering. Previous works have primarily constructed computer-use environments manually by interacting with an actual operating system, and then taken VM snapshots to be reused later [53]. However, snapshots cannot be inspected, version-controlled, or partially reused across tasks, and modifying anything requires repeating the manual setup, therefore limiting modularity, reproducibility, and scalability. To handle these challenges, we construct theGym-Anything图书馆。在Library. InGym-Anything，每个环境都由一个简单的规范定义：三个连续的安装脚本和一个声明性配置文件。脚本从一般性进展到特定于任务：, each environment is defined by a simple specification: three sequential setup scripts and a declarative configuration file. The scripts progress from general to task-specific:安装install安装软件及其依赖项，installs the software and its dependencies,配置configure使用实际数据和设置进行设置，并且sets it up with realistic data and settings, and任务设置task setup配置给定任务的特定启动状态。这种分离确保同一软件的多个任务共享相同的安装和配置脚本，仅改变特定于任务的设置。例如，创建 LibreOffice Calc 环境仅需要一个安装脚本（例如，configures the specific starting state for a given task. This separation ensures that multiple tasks for the same software share the same install and configure scripts, varying only the task-specific setup. For example, creating a LibreOffice Calc environment requires only an install script (e.g.,apt-get install libreoffice）、下载示例电子表格的配置脚本以及指定操作系统映像和资源限制的配置文件；该库自动处理容器编排、显示转发和检查点管理。这种设计将环境创建简化为脚本任务：用户和 AI 代理通过编写设置脚本和键值配置来创建新环境，然后通过标准体育馆式 API [44, 8] 与任何环境进行交互，该 API 提供统一的观察空间（例如屏幕截图）和操作空间（例如键盘和鼠标输入），库处理跨平台的显示转发和输入翻译), a configure script that downloads a sample spreadsheet, and a config file specifying the OS image and resource limits; the library handles container orchestration, display forwarding, and checkpoint management automatically. This design reduces environment creation to a scripting task: users and AI agents create new environments by writing setup scripts and key-value configurations, then interact with any environment through a standard gymnasium-style API [44, 8] that provides a unified observation space (e.g., screenshots) and action space (e.g., keyboard and mouse inputs), with the library handling display forwarding and input translation across

图4：Figure 4:的The Gym-Anything 创建-审核循环。creation-audit loop.创建代理编写设置脚本并生成证据文档（屏幕截图、日志等），而审核代理根据质量检查表评估此证据并返回反馈。所学知识积累在共享记忆中A Creation Agent writes setup scripts and produces evidence documents (screenshots, logs, etc.) while an Audit Agent evaluates this evidence against quality checklists and returns feedback. Learnings accumulate in a shared memoryM，汇总代理定期对其进行压缩，以便更快地创建新环境。, which a Summarization Agent periodically condenses so that newer environments are created faster.

操作系统。该规范非常简单，LLM 代理可以自主创作环境（§3），但表达能力足以捕获配置有实际数据的复杂的生产级软件，范围从桌面图像编辑器到多容器企业系统。在这个简单的界面背后，该库管理跨三个操作系统（Linux、Windows、Android）和多个计算后端（例如用于 slurm 等无根系统的 docker 和 apptainer）的运行环境的复杂性。分阶段设计进一步支持在每个阶段边界进行缓存，因此创建新任务只需要重新运行特定于任务的设置。与网络-进程-文件隔离相结合，可以实现大规模并行化；在我们的实验中，我们在 1,600 个 CPU 上运行 400 多个并发环境（附录 B）。operating systems. This specification is simple enough that an LLM agent can author environments autonomously (§3), yet expressive enough to capture complex, production-grade software configured with realistic data, ranging from desktop image editors to multi-container enterprise systems. Behind this simple interface, the library manages the complexity of running environments across three operating systems (Linux, Windows, Android) and multiple compute backends (such as docker and apptainer for rootless systems such as slurm). The staged design further enables caching at each stage boundary, so creating new tasks only requires re-running the task-specific setup. Combined with network-process-file isolation, this enables massive parallelization; in our experiments, we run 400+ concurrent environments across 1,600 CPUs (Appendix B).

3 扩展计算机使用代理软件应用程序3 Scaling Computer-Use Agent Software Applications

即使对于专家来说，将现实世界的软件设置为可交互的环境也是困难、费力且耗时的[53,56,20]。每个软件都需要安装、使用适当的数据进行配置以及验证；例如，放射学工具需要带注释的临床 CT 扫描，而 ERP 系统需要交易历史和供应商帐户。每个应用程序通常需要数周的专家努力，自然限制了可扩展性。Setting up real-world software as interactable environments is hard, laborious, and time-consuming, even for expert humans [53, 56, 20]. Each software requires installation, configuration with domainappropriate data, and verification; for instance, a radiology tool requires annotated clinical CT scans, while an ERP system needs transaction histories and vendor accounts. This often demands weeks of expert effort per application, naturally limiting scalability.

关键的想法是The key idea is that设置计算机使用代理环境本身就是一项编码+计算机使用代理任务。setting up computer-use agent environments is itself a coding + computer-use agent task.由于 Gym-Anything 库（第 2.3 节）将环境创建限制在固定的小型界面（编写设置脚本和配置文件），因此创建任务变成了编码任务。此外，验证环境是否正确设置需要启动它并与其交互，这是一项计算机使用代理任务。Because the Gym-Anything library (§2.3) constrains environment creation to a fixed, small interface (writing setup scripts and config files), the creation task becomes a coding task. Further, verifying whether the environment is correctly set up requires launching it and interacting with it, which is a computer-use agent task.

然而，即使是最先进的代理，天真地提示也会导致恶劣的环境；代理提前停止，使用虚假的占位符数据，将软件留在错误的启动屏幕，或者声称事情已经完成而没有实际验证。因此，我们提出了一个多代理框架，它可以迭代地创建、审核和改进环境，同时在共享内存中积累学习内容。However, naively prompting even state-of-the-art agents results in poor environments; the agent stops early, uses fake placeholder data, leaves the software at the wrong starting screen, or claims things are done without actually verifying them. We therefore propose a multi-agent framework that iteratively creates, audits, and improves environments, while accumulating learnings in a shared memory.

多代理框架。Multi-agent framework.我们框架中的每个代理都是通过 Claude Code 运行的 Claude Opus 4.5/4.6 [3] 的实例，其区别在于 a.) 对特定工具的访问，以及 b.) 系统提示所描述的目标。简而言之，这些代理迭代地生成环境，审核质量并改进它们，并在共享内存中记录未来尝试的学习内容Each agent in our framework is an instance of Claude Opus 4.5/4.6 [3] run via Claude Code, differentiated by a.) access to specific tools, and b.) the objective described by its system prompt. In a nutshell, these agents iteratively generate environments, audit the quality and improve them, and document the learnings for future attempts in a shared memoryM。接下来我们详细描述这 3 个代理。. We next describe each of the 3 agents in detail.

创作代理（AgentCreation agent (Agent C ).这是一个编码代理，配备了 bash、python 和计算机使用（用于视觉基础）工具，可以完全访问This is a coding agent equipped with bash, python, and computer-use (for visual grounding) tools, with complete access to theGym-Anything库和所有以前创建的library and all previously created

6

环境。给定一个新的软件名称environments. Given a new software nameSk，我们提示Agent, we prompt AgentC具有与软件无关的详细提示，描述要遵循的工作流程和库的使用情况，以及with a software-agnostic detailed prompt describing the workflow to follow and library usage, along withSk，其目标是将软件实现为一个环境，然后通过实际运行并与其交互来验证它。在编写任何脚本之前，代理首先研究如何配置软件，查找并下载环境的真实数据（例如，放射学软件的公共医学成像数据集、消息客户端的已发布电子邮件语料库），并研究以前创建的类似环境。然后，它实现设置脚本、启动环境、截取屏幕截图、使用视觉基础来检查应用程序是否达到了预期状态（如设置脚本所预期的那样），并迭代地调试故障。至关重要的是，代理需要以运行软件的屏幕截图、执行日志等形式提供软件设置正确的证据（示例请参见附录 D）。, with the objective of implementing the software as an environment and then verifying it by actually running and interacting with it. Before writing any scripts, the agent first researches how the software should be configured, finds and downloads real-world data for the environment (e.g., public medical imaging datasets for radiology software, published email corpora for messaging clients), and studies similar previously created environments. It then implements the setup scripts, launches the environment, takes screenshots, uses visual grounding to check that the application reached the expected state (as intended by the setup scripts), and iteratively debugs failures. Crucially, the agent is required to produce evidence that the software was set up correctly in the form of screenshots of the running software, execution logs, etc. (see Appendix D for an example).

然而，代理常常过早地宣布任务完成。例如，它可能使用占位符数据而不是真实数据集，将软件留在错误的屏幕上，或者从不通过实际执行来验证任务。我们推测这些失败是由于上下文疲劳 [27, 39] 造成的：在数十万个令牌之后，代理不知道它仍然需要做什么。为了解决这个问题，每当代理停止时，我们都会重新提示它重新阅读设置指南，重新阅读清单，并完成它可能跳过的任何要求。我们发现这种简单的技术可以弥补许多遗漏。However, the agent often declares the task done prematurely. For instance, it may use placeholder data instead of real datasets, leave the software at the wrong screen, or never verify the task by actual execution. We speculate that these failures are due to context fatigue [27, 39]: after hundreds of thousands of tokens, the agent loses track of what it still needs to do. To address this, whenever the agent stops, we re-prompt it to reread the setup guidelines, reread the checklists, and complete any requirements it may have skipped. We find this simple technique recovers many omissions.

审计代理（Agentaudit）。Audit agent (Agentaudit).代理期间While AgentC通常情况下，环境会运行起来，但其关于其所做操作的声明并不总是可靠的。例如，它可能会将软件保留在设置向导而不是主屏幕上，使用占位符数据，或者完全跳过验证。然而，无论代理声称什么，上面产生的证据都揭示了环境的实际状态：屏幕截图显示软件是否正常运行或卡在错误屏幕上。typically gets the environment running, its claims about what it has done are not always reliable. For instance, it may leave the software at a setup wizard instead of the main screen, use placeholder data, or skip verification entirely. However, the evidence produced above reveals the actual state of the environment regardless of what the agent claims: a screenshot shows whether the software is running correctly or stuck on an error screen.

为了验证这一证据，我们使用 Agentaudit，一个类似编码+计算机使用的代理，充当 Agent 的对手To verify this evidence, we use Agentaudit, a similar coding+computer-use agent that acts as an adversary to AgentC并评估证据是否表明环境满足一组质量检查表（参见附录 D）。它通过分析屏幕截图和日志、检查实际的配置和脚本文件，并在必要时实际运行环境来实现这一点。鉴于软件的实现and evaluates whether the evidence demonstrates that the environment satisfies a set of quality checklists (see Appendix D). It does so by analyzing the screenshots and logs, inspecting the actual config and script files, and, if necessary, actually running the environment. Given the implementation of softwareSk，审计代理输出审计，详细说明正确实施的内容以及关键问题是什么（附录 E 包含示例审计，附录 E.4 显示如何在审计轮次中纠正问题）。, the audit agent outputs an audit detailing what is correctly implemented and what the critical issues are (Appendix E contains example audits, and Appendix E.4 shows how issues are corrected across audit rounds).

原则上双方代理In principle, both AgentC和 Agentaudit 可以访问相同的工具和文件；唯一的区别在于它们的提示。我们发现这种分离具有多种好处：a.）分离代理消除了任何自我确认偏差，并且我们发现审核比自我审查更详细和准确（第7.4节），b.）书面审核确保了更高的可解释性，让人类作者独立验证质量（附录E），以及c.）对抗性框架捕获了代理的情况and Agentaudit have access to the same tools and files; the only difference lies in their prompt. We find this separation offers multiple benefits: a.) separating agents removes any self-confirmation bias, and we find audits are more detailed and accurate than self-review (§7.4), b.) the written audits ensure higher interpretability, letting human authors independently verify quality (Appendix E), and c.) the adversarial framing catches cases where AgentC做出自我误导的主张。审核结果反馈给代理made self-misleading claims. The audit findings are fed back to AgentC进行校正，并且该循环运行for correction, and this loop runs fort迭代。我们框架的一个关键特征是代理在共享内存中积累学习内容，使它们能够在迭代中不断改进。我们接下来描述这个机制。iterations. A key feature of our framework is that agents accumulate learnings in a shared memory, allowing them to improve over iterations. We describe this mechanism next.

共享内存。Shared memory.创建代理维护共享内存The creation agent maintains a shared memoryM，实际上是一个随着时间的推移而增长的文件目录。, effectively a directory of files that grows over time.M使用 Agent 的手写提示进行初始化is initialized with the hand-written prompt for AgentC（描述设置工作流程、验证清单和库使用）并随着代理添加他们的知识而不断发展。每个环境之后，Agent(describing the setup workflow, checklists for verification, and library usage) and evolves as agents add their learnings. After each environment, AgentC记录它尝试过的内容、失败的内容以及修复的内容，更新documents what it tried, what failed, and what fixed it, updatingM在两个地方：软件特定注释in two places: software-specific notesM柔和而一般的音符soft and general notesM一般可以帮助未来的代理商。例如，一名代理发现多服务 Web 平台需要在 GUI 启动之前进行就绪轮询；一旦添加到general that could help future agents. For instance, one agent discovered that a multi-service web platform needed readiness polling before the GUI could launch; once added toM，它成为所有后续 Web 堆栈的默认设置，从而加快了创建速度。这确保了创建时间的非线性增长：随着构建更多环境，可以更快地创建更新的环境。此外，, it became the default for all subsequent web stacks, resulting in faster creation. This ensures sublinear growth in creation time: as more environments are built, newer environments can be created faster. Further,M充当异步但共享的内存，以便并行运行的多个代理可以写入和读取其他代理的发现。然而，随着更多环境的创建，acts as an asynchronous but shared memory, such that multiple agents running in parallel can write to and read from discoveries made by other agents. However, as more environments are created,Msoft 变得很大，导致未来的智能体由于长上下文而错过重要的细节。为了解决这个问题，在每个soft grows large, causing future agents to miss important details due to long contexts. To address this, in everyL环境中，内存摘要代理 (Agentsumm) 会读取所有内存文件，找到常见模式，并总结发现的结果environment, a memory summarization agent (Agentsumm) reads through all memory files, finds common patterns, and summarizes findings fromM软入soft intoM一般。理论上这仅增加general. This theoretically adds only∼ 1 /L与每个代理读取完整内容相比的开销overhead compared to each agent reading the fullM每次都很软。soft every time.

输出。Output.将此秘诀应用于通过我们基于 GDP 的选择（第 2.2 节）确定的软件，我们为跨三个操作系统（Linux、Windows、Android）的 200 个软件应用程序构建了环境，从桌面应用程序到多服务企业系统，每个环境都配置了真实的数据（公共电子邮件语料库、医学成像数据集、财务模式、Applying this recipe to the software identified through our GDP-grounded selection (§2.2), we construct environments for 200 software applications across three operating systems (Linux, Windows, Android), ranging from desktop applications to multi-service enterprise systems, each configured with realistic data (public email corpora, medical imaging datasets, financial schemas,

和政府开放数据）。我们根据当前的计算预算选择 200；该管道是完全自动化的，并且可以扩展到其他软件。接下来我们将描述如何为这些环境生成任务。and government open data). We select 200 based on our current compute budget; the pipeline is fully automated and extensible to additional software. We next describe how tasks are generated for these environments.

4 扩展任务4 Scaling Tasks

虽然§3解决了使复杂软件正确运行的主要瓶颈（处理安装、配置和后台服务），但在这些环境中生成不同的任务带来了单独的扩展挑战。回想一下§2.1，任务需要一个起始环境状态While §3 addressed the primary bottleneck of getting complex software to run correctly (handling installation, configuration, and background services), generating diverse tasks over these environments poses a separate scaling challenge. Recall from §2.1 that a task requires a starting environment stateE ( s0), 自然语言指令0), a natural-language instructionp，和一个验证者, and a verifierV。配置基础软件后，创建新任务就减少为生成这些特定于任务的资产。. Once the base software is configured, creating new tasks reduces to generating these task-specific assets.

尽管如此，天真地促使模型生成这些通常会导致质量不佳。例如，设置脚本引用不存在的数据，格式与软件的期望不匹配，指令要么非常简单，要么不可能从给定的起始状态执行。相反，纯粹依靠代理模型来编写和验证每项任务对于扩展而言成本高昂。Nonetheless, naively prompting a model to generate these often results in subpar quality. For instance, setup scripts reference non-existent data, formats mismatch the software’s expectations, and instructions are either trivially simple or impossible to execute from the given starting state. Conversely, relying purely on agentic models to author and validate every task is prohibitively expensive for scaling.

为了有效地扩展任务创建，我们提出了To scale task creation efficiently, we propose a提出并扩大propose-and-amplify策略。首先，提案者代理（Claude Opus 4.5/4.6，通过 Claude Code，配备计算机使用工具）提出一小组高质量、困难的提案。strategy. First, a proposer agent (Claude Opus 4.5/4.6 via Claude Code, equipped with computer-use tools) proposes a small set of high quality, difficult种子任务seed tasks每个软件。为代理提供了一系列跨越三个维度的高质量任务指南：a.）现实性（指令是否反映了真实的、现实世界的用例？），b.）难度（任务是否需要长期、多步骤的轨迹来解决？），c.）多样性（任务是否涵盖软件的不同功能？）。这里需要一个代理循环，因为模型必须主动运行软件、搜索、下载或生成真实数据、通过 GUI 交互并验证结果状态。至关重要的是，这个昂贵的步骤每个软件只发生一次，确保涵盖第 2.2 节中确定的相关职业的核心功能。per software. The agent is provided with a set of guidelines for high quality tasks across three dimensions: a.) realism (does the instruction reflect a genuine, real-world use case?), b.) difficulty (does the task require a long-horizon, multi-step trajectory to solve?), and c.) diversity (do the tasks cover varied functionalities of the software?). An agentic loop is necessary here because the model must actively run the software, search, download or generate realistic data, interact via the GUI, and verify the resulting state. Crucially, this expensive step only occurs once per software, ensuring core functionality across relevant occupations identified in §2.2 is covered.

其次，为了放大，非代理法学硕士 (Gemini 3 Pro) 使用这些高质量种子作为上下文示例来大规模生成其他任务。虽然代理种子确保了进一步生成的任务的真实性和难度，但从非代理法学硕士中天真地采样通常会产生重复或非常相似的指令。为了加强多样性，我们按顺序生成任务，为模型提供所有先前生成的指令 1Second, for amplification, a non-agentic LLM (Gemini 3 Pro) uses these high-quality seeds as in-context examples to generate additional tasks at scale. While the agentic seeds ensure realism and difficulty in further generated tasks, naively sampling from a non-agentic LLM often yields repetitive or very similar instructions. To enforce diversity, we generate tasks sequentially, providing the model with all previously generated instructions 1, . 。 。 , t, . . . , t作为任务的上下文as context for taskt+1。我们随后应用语义相似性过滤来丢弃重复的任务。+1. We subsequently apply semantic similarity filtering to discard duplicate tasks.

最后，由于非代理LLM生成任务时无需交互执行，因此我们实现了自动过滤步骤。我们启动每个生成的任务，捕获起始状态观察Finally, because the non-agentic LLM generates tasks without interactive execution, we implement an automated filtering step. We launch each generated task, capture the starting state observationo0，并将其与指令一起传递给视觉语言模型（VLM），以检查开始状态是否与任务描述的期望相匹配。未通过此测试的任务将从数据集中过滤掉。附录 G 中提供了任务描述和起始状态的示例。0, and pass it alongside the instruction to a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to check whether the start state matches the expectation from the task description. Tasks that fail this test are filtered from the dataset. Examples of task descriptions and starting states are provided in Appendix G.

4.1 任务验证4.1 Task Verification

回想一下每个任务Recall that each taskT=（= (Es 0 , p, V) 包括验证功能) includes a verification functionV将智能体的轨迹映射到分数（§2.1）。评估长期轨迹需要that maps the agent’s trajectory to a score (§2.1). 特权信息。Privileged information.每个任务的起始状态Each task’s starting states0 由安装脚本配置0 is configured by the setup scriptsS（第 2.3 节中的安装、配置和任务设置脚本）。这些脚本包含任务描述中不存在的真实数据(the install, configure, and task setup scripts from §2.3). These scripts contain ground-truth data that is not present in the task descriptionp但确定性地与环境的配置相关。我们称之为but is deterministically tied to the environment’s configuration. We call this特权信息我privileged information I= 提取（= Extract(S、pS, p），由单独的编码代理自动提取，该代理解析脚本、检索或在线搜索相关的基本事实。例如，在医学成像任务中，从下载的数据集中已经知道正确的肿瘤位置；在财务任务中，预期的账户余额是由初始化数据决定的。重要的是，), extracted automatically by a separate coding agent that parses the scripts, retrieves, or searches online for the relevant ground truth. For instance, in a medical imaging task, the correct tumor location is already known from the downloaded dataset; in a financial task, the expected account balances are determined by the initialization data. 其中每个 VLM(where each VLM(τ,ci,我τ, ci, I) 返回是否为子任务的二元判断) returns a binary judgment of whether subtaskci已完成，使用was completed, usingI根据已知的真实答案检查代理的输出。该公式允许对复杂的多步骤任务进行部分计分，而无需手动注释。表 1 显示了特权信息的代表性示例以及它在科学分析、临床报告、业务运营和临床决策支持中实现的具体清单项目。to check the agent’s outputs against known ground-truth answers. This formulation allows partial credit on complex, multi-step tasks without requiring manual annotation. Table 1 shows representative examples of privileged information and the concrete checklist items it enables across scientific analysis, clinical reporting, business operations, and clinical decision support.

除了In addition toC，VLM验证器评估单独的完整性检查表, the VLM verifier evaluates a separate integrity checklistC确保评估的计算机使用代理不会绕过预期的工作流程：a.）使用预期的软件来完成任务，b.）通过软件自己的界面而不是直接编辑配置或数据文件达到所需的应用程序状态，以及c.）代理没有利用环境工件来简化工作。任何完整性项目集均未通过int to ensure the evaluated computer-use agent did not bypass the intended workflow: a.) the intended software was used to complete the task, b.) the required application state was reached through the software’s own interface rather than by directly editing configuration or data files, and c.) the agent did not exploit environment artifacts to shortcut the work. Failing any integrity item setsV ( τ) = 0，无论任务清单分数如何。我们手动比较了基于清单的 VLM 验证与仅最终状态的 VLM 验证和程序化验证的人工同意率，发现所提出的方法明显更可靠（第 7.3 节）。) = 0, regardless of the task checklist score. We manually compared human-agreement rates of our checklist-based VLM verification against end-state-only VLM verification and programmatic verification, finding the proposed method to be significantly more reliable (§7.3).

5 CUA-World

正在申请ApplyingGym-Anything根据我们的计算预算，提议者为每个软件生成 5 个任务，放大器生成 75 个任务。过滤后，会产生跨 200 个软件应用程序的 12,103 个任务和环境，每个任务和环境都具有基于清单的验证（表 2；图 5）。如表2所示，with our compute budget, the proposer generates 5 and the amplifier 75 tasks per piece of software. After filtering, this yields 12,103 tasks and environments across 200 software applications, each with checklist-based verification (Table 2; Figure 5). As shown in Table 2,CUA-World是第一个同时提供大规模交互式环境（200 多种软件、10K+ 任务）、支持长期评估、涵盖所有 22 个主要职业组、提供自动化环境创建并包括训练分割的集合。我们分is the first collection to simultaneously provide interactive environments at scale (200+ varieties of software, 10K+ tasks), support long-horizon evaluation, cover all 22 major occupation groups, offer automated environment creation, and include a training split. We divideCUA-World分为训练和测试部分。into Train and Test splits.

图5：Figure 5:定量比较Quantitative comparison of CUA-World 对照现有基准against existing benchmarks跨越四个维度。前两个轴使用对数刻度。across four dimensions. The first two axes use a log scale.

污染物过滤。Contamination filtering.为了确保拆分之间没有数据泄漏，我们应用保守的污染检查。给定两个任务指令，我们提示法学硕士按照 1 到 8 的范围对它们的相似性进行评分（范围从“不相似”到“重复、子集或超集”）。任何得分为 4（“非常相似”）或更高的配对都被标记为受污染。我们通过将任务视为节点并将污染标志视为相似图中的边来形式化这一点。我们计算该图的连接组件，并将整个组件随机分配给训练或测试分割，确保跨分割的两个任务不会相互污染。手动验证显示管道是适当保守的：它标记了几个未受污染的对（误报），但错过了很少的真实污染实例（漏报）。有关更多详细信息，请参阅附录 H。To ensure no data leakage between splits, we apply a conservative contamination check. Given two task instructions, we prompt an LLM to grade their similarity on a scale of 1 to 8 (ranging from “not similar” to “duplicate, subset, or superset”). Any pair scoring 4 (“very similar”) or higher is flagged as contaminated. We formalize this by treating tasks as nodes and contamination flags as edges in a similarity graph. We compute the connected components of this graph and randomly assign entire components to either the Train or Test split, ensuring no two tasks across splits contaminate each other. Manual verification shows the pipeline is suitably conservative: it flags several non-contaminated pairs (false positives) but misses very few true instances of contamination (false negatives). For more details, see Appendix H.

10

CUA-World -长。-Long.为了评估超长期任务的智能体，我们引入To evaluate agents on extremely long-horizon tasks, we introduceCUA-World- 长，一组 200 个任务（每个软件一个）。关键的挑战是生成比基准测试中已有的任务更难的任务，同时又保持可解决性。我们通过轨迹引导策略来解决这个问题：对于每个软件，我们首先生成- Long, a set of 200 tasks (one per software). The key challenge is generating tasks that are genuinely harder than those already in the benchmark while remaining solvable. We address this with a trajectory-guided strategy: for each piece of software, we first generatek来自强大的计算机使用代理对现有任务的轨迹，然后提示编码和视觉代理分析这些轨迹，特别确定为什么某些任务的通过率较低，并注意到常见的失败模式。代理还收到一套 8 条质量指南，涵盖现实世界的相关性、客观可评估性、现实数据等（参见附录 F.1）。trajectories from a strong computer-use agent on existing tasks, then prompt a coding and visual agent to analyze these trajectories, specifically identifying why certain tasks have lower pass rates and noting common failure modes. The agent also receives a set of 8 quality guidelines covering real-world relevance, objective evaluability, realistic data, and others (see Appendix F.1).

根据此分析，代理会创建一个比该软件应用程序现有任务更难的新任务。虽然代理的失败评估并不完美，但最终的任务却要困难得多。我们根据 8 项质量标准手动验证所有 200 项任务是否设置正确且有意义。此外，对于未通过此验证的任务，我们通过与代理的进一步交互来迭代地完善它们。完整的流程在附录 F.2 中描述。这些任务通常需要人类完成 200 多个步骤，而当前的模型即使在 500 个步骤之后也经常失败。Based on this analysis, the agent creates a new task designed to be harder than the existing ones for that software application. While the agent’s failure assessment is not perfect, the resulting tasks are substantially more difficult. We manually verify that all 200 tasks are set up correctly and are meaningful according to the 8 quality criteria. Further for tasks that fail this verification, we iteratively refine them through further interaction with the agent. The full pipeline is described in Appendix F.2. These tasks often require more than 200 steps for human completion, and current models frequently fail even after 500 steps.

6 实验装置6 Experimental Setup

接下来我们描述一下我们如何使用We next describe how we useCUA-World有两个作用：作为提取较小模型的训练数据源，以及作为计算机使用代理的评估基准。in two roles: as a source of training data for distilling smaller models, and as an evaluation benchmark for computer-use agents.

6.1 培训6.1 Training

评估效用To evaluate the utility ofCUA-World-训练，我们将执行轨迹从强大的教师模型（Kimi-K 2.5 [43]）提炼为较小的学生模型（Qwen3-VL-2B-Thinking [58]）。对于训练分组中的每个任务，我们生成-Train, we distill execution trajectories from a strong teacher model (Kimi-K 2.5 [43]) into a smaller student model (Qwen3-VL-2B-Thinking [58]). For every task in the training split, we generatek= 4 个来自教师的轨迹，直到至少一个是正确的，并利用这些成功的推出进行微调。我们总共收集了所有 10,000 项任务的大约 2000 条轨迹。= 4 trajectories from the teacher until at least one is correct, and utilize these successful rollouts for fine-tuning. Cumulatively, we collect roughly 2000 trajectories across all 10,000 tasks.

我们进一步在一小部分软件上系统地消除了这个蒸馏过程中的几个设计选择，研究：（1）教师模型选择，（2）每个轨迹的最佳步数和样本数（结果参见§7.4）。然后，我们使用最佳配置在所有轨迹上提取我们的模型。蒸馏后，我们评估模型We further systematically ablate several design choices in this distillation process on a small set of software, investigating: (1) teacher model selection, (2) the optimal number of steps and samples per trajectory (see §7.4 for results). We then use the best configuration to distill our model on all trajectories. Post-distillation, we evaluate the models onCUA-World-与 OSWorld [53] 等外部基准测试一起进行测试。-Test alongside external benchmarks such as OSWorld [53].

6.2 测试时审计（TTA）代理6.2 Test-Time Auditing (TTA) Agent

中的一些任务Some of the tasks inCUA-World非常长。这为测试代理是否能够在更广泛的范围内工作提供了独特的机会。然而，我们发现当前的智能体经常在几十个步骤后停止，犯错误或过早地声称任务尚未完成。are extremely long. This opens up a unique opportunity to test agents capable of working over extended horizons. However, we find that current agents often stop after a few dozen steps, making mistakes or prematurely claiming the task is complete when it is not.

受到我们软件生成方法（§3）的启发，我们引入了审计代理来解决这个问题。每当主模型发出任务终止信号时，我们就会运行此审计代理。它将完整的轨迹（所有屏幕截图）作为输入并确定任务是否真正完成。至关重要的是，它没有接收来自主模型的思想链，因为我们发现这会使审计师的评估产生偏差。如果审计代理确定任务未完成，它会生成对缺失内容的解释。我们将此反馈提供给主要的计算机使用代理，促使其继续完成任务。Inspired by our approach in software generation (§3), we introduce an audit agent to address this. Whenever the main model signals that the task is terminated, we run this audit agent. It takes the complete trajectory (all screenshots) as input and determines whether the task is actually complete. Crucially, it does not receive the chain-of-thought from the main model, as we find this biases the auditor’s assessment. If the audit agent determines that the task is not completed, it generates an explanation of what is missing. We provide this feedback back to the main computer-use agent, prompting it to continue completing the task.

6.3 评价6.3 Evaluation

我们使用第 4.1 节中描述的基于清单的 VLM 验证程序来评估代理。每个任务的清单由加权子任务组成；我们报告两个指标：(1)We evaluate agents using the checklist-based VLM verifier described in §4.1. Each task’s checklist consists of weighted subtasks; we report two metrics: (1)平均分Average Score(0-100)，跨任务的平均检查表分数，获得部分学分，以及 (2)(0-100), the mean checklist score across tasks, which captures partial credit, and (2)通过率Pass Rate(%)，完全完成任务的比例，即达到完美的清单分数。我们评估的是(%), the fraction of tasks fully completed, i.e., achieving a perfect checklist score. We evaluate onCUA-World-测试（完整的测试分割）和-Test (the full test split) andCUA-World-Long（200 个长期任务，每个软件一个）。除非另有说明，我们使用 Gemini 3 Flash 作为 VLM 验证器。每个特工每集都有一个最大预算：200 步-Long (200 long-horizon tasks, one per software). Unless otherwise noted, we use Gemini 3 Flash as the VLM verifier. Each agent is given a maximum budget per episode: 200 steps forCUA-World- 测试，500 步或 5 美元，以先达到者为准-Test, and 500 steps or $5, whichever hits first, forCUA-World-长。对于 GPT-5.4 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6，我们使用他们的官方代理工具。适用于 Gemini 3 Flash 和 Kimi-K-Long. For GPT-5.4 and Claude Sonnet 4.6, we use their official agent harnesses. For Gemini 3 Flash and Kimi-K

11

2.5，在我们进行实验时，官方工具尚未公开，因此我们采用了 Qwen3-VL 工具（附录 K.2）。2.5, official harnesses were not publicly available at the time of our experiments, so we adapted the Qwen3-VL harness (Appendix K.2).

CUA-World -长对于前沿模型来说具有挑战性。-Long is challenging for frontier models.表 4 显示了多个前沿模型的性能Table 4 shows the performance of multiple frontier models onCUA-World-长。即使是最强的模型Gemini-3-Flash，通过率也仅为7.5%，平均分为36.2。有趣的是，GPT5.4 的通过率达到 3%，而 Claude-Sonnet-4.6 的通过率达到 6%。部分原因是他们在大约 150 个步骤中耗尽了 5 美元的预算（-Long. Even the strongest model, Gemini-3-Flash, achieves a pass rate of only 7.5% and an average score of 36.2. Interestingly, GPT5.4 achieves 3% pass rate while Claude-Sonnet-4.6 achieves 6%. This is partly because they exhaust their $5 budget in roughly 150 steps (≤GPT-5.4 为 100），比 Gemini-3-Flash 少得多。为了测试预算是否是瓶颈，我们取消了成本上限，并将 GPT-5.4 和 Gemini-3Flash 的步数限制提高到 2,000（表 4，下半部分）。两个模型都有很大改进，特别是 GPT-5.4 达到了 27.5% 的通过率。100 for GPT-5.4), much less than Gemini-3-Flash. To test whether budget is a bottleneck, we remove the cost cap and raise the step limit to 2,000 for GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3Flash (Table 4, lower half). Both models substantially improve, notably GPT-5.4 reaching 27.5% pass rate.

然而，这些改进需要大量的测试时间成本。平均而言，Gemini-3-Flash 需要 1,300 步，每条轨迹大约 16 美元，而 GPT-5.4 需要 242 步，每条轨迹大约 18 美元。这些结果强调，在智能体能够可靠、高效地处理长期、多步骤的工作流程之前，需要改进模型功能。However, these improvements come at a substantial test-time cost. On average, Gemini-3-Flash requires 1,300 steps and approximately $16 per trajectory, while GPT-5.4 requires 242 steps and approximately $18 per trajectory. These results highlight that improvements in model capabilities are needed before agents can reliably and efficiently handle the long-horizon, multi-step workflows thatCUA-World-长期要求。-Long demands.

扩展软件应用程序和环境：性能随着软件和任务数量的增加而扩展。Scaling Software Applications and Environments: Performance scales with both increasing software and task count.图 6a 显示了精炼 2B 模型的得分情况Figure 6a shows how the distilled 2B model’s score onCUA-World-当我们沿着两个轴缩放训练数据时，测试会发生变化：软件应用程序的数量（50、100、200），同时保留每个软件的所有任务，以及所有 200 个软件应用程序的任务比例（25%、50%、100%）。两条曲线都显示出一致的性能改进，遵循大致对数线性趋势-Test changes as we scale the training data along two axes: the number of software applications (50, 100, 200) while keeping all tasks per software, and the fraction of tasks (25%, 50%, 100%) across all 200 software applications. Both curves show consistent performance improvements, following a roughly log-linear trend of∼数据加倍时增加 3.5 个点。这表明进一步扩大我们的3.5 point increase on doubling the data. This suggests further scaling ourGym-Anythingpipeline 可以产生一个更强大的模型。pipeline could yield an even stronger model.

概括：蒸馏可以提高已见和未见软件的性能，但在已见软件上获得的收益更大。Generalization: Distillation improves performance on both seen and unseen software, but gains are larger on seen software.为了研究蒸馏如何推广到训练期间未见过的软件，我们在 200 个软件应用程序中的 25% 和 50% 上训练模型，并分别评估训练期间使用的软件 (IID) 和未使用的软件 (OOD)（图 7）。两者的性能均有所提高：在 IID 软件上，平均分数从 16.7 增加到 24.2（占软件的 25%），在 OOD 软件上，平均分数从 12.3 增加到 14.1。然而，OOD增益是有限的；图 7 显示，它仅恢复了所有软件培训所获得的改进的 22-27%，而 IID 软件则为 65-87%。这表明对未见过的软件的泛化确实发生了，但只是To study how distillation generalizes to software not seen during training, we train models on 25% and 50% of the 200 software applications and evaluate separately on the software used during training (IID) and those that are not used (OOD) (Figure 7). Performance improves on both: on IID software, the average score increases from 16.7 to 24.2 (at 25% of software), and on OOD software from 12.3 to 14.1. However, the OOD gain is limited; Figure 7 shows it recovers only 22-27% of the improvement one would obtain from training on all software, compared to 65-87% on IID software. This suggests that generalization to unseen software does happen but is

Each bar spans from the untrained baseline (bottom) to the model trained on all software (top). The solid portion shows the gain recovered by the model trained on the subset; the hatched portion shows the remaining gap. Training on a subset recovers 65-87% of the gain on IID software but only 22-27% on OOD software, indicating that generalization to unseen software is limited and scaling to diverse software is important.

有限。其次，由于 IID 软件的恢复范围为 65%-87%，这意味着对特定软件的培训有很大帮助，但对其他软件的培训也有助于评估软件的性能。总体而言，这强调了为数字经济中使用的各种软件构建代理需要针对特定软件且跨多种软件的培训，从而激发了对可扩展环境创建管道的需求，例如limited. Secondly, since recovery on IID software ranges from 65%-87% this implies training on a specific software helps substantially, but training on other software also contributes in the evaluated software’s performance. Overall, this underscores that building agents for the large variety of software used in the digital economy requires training that is both software-specific and across a diverse set of software, motivating the need for scalable environment creation pipelines such asGym-Anything。更大的模型可能会更好地泛化到软件中，我们将其留给未来的工作。. Larger models may generalize better across software, which we leave to future work.

通过率随步骤预算而变化，测试时间审核可提供进一步的收益。Pass rate scales with step budget, and Test-Time Auditing provides further gains.图 6b 显示了 Gemini 3 Flash 的通过率Figure 6b shows how Gemini 3 Flash’s pass rate onCUA-World-随着我们增加每集的最大步数预算，每个点代表 50、100、200、500、2,000 步的预算，因此发生了长的变化。通过率在平均 50 到 100 步之间保持较低水平 (2.0%-Long changes as we increase the maximum step budget per episode, where each point represents a budget of 50, 100, 200, 500, 2,000 steps. Pass rate stays low between 50 and 100 average steps (2.0%→2.5%），然后在较高的预算下急剧上升（6.5%2.5%), then rises steeply at higher budgets (6.5% at∼200 步，7.5%200 steps, 7.5% at∼400 步）。急剧的跳跃可能表明大多数400 steps). CUA-World-长任务需要最少的步骤（-Long tasks require a minimum number of steps (>100) 在代理完成之前。增加计算量超出此范围继续提供帮助，达到 11.5%100) before the agent can complete them at all. Further, the TTA agent uplifts performance even further, raising the pass rate to 14.0% under the same 2,000-step cap. Since the maximum step budget remains the same, this likely implies that TTA helps when the model stops prematurely, as the auditor is able to verify the trajectory and provide feedback on any missed subtasks.

轨迹行为模式：失败的轨迹主要是重试循环，而成功的轨迹则更频繁地验证其进度。Trajectory Behavioral Patterns: Failed trajectories are dominated by retry loops, while successful ones verify their progress more often.了解代理的行为方式To understand how agents behave onCUA-World，我们分析了 Gemini-3-Flash 评估的所有轨迹, we analyze all trajectories from Gemini-3-Flash evaluated onCUA-World，使用自动行为分析管道（附录 M）。我们在这里重点关注 Gemini-3-Flash，并注意不同型号的模式可能有所不同。我们首先通过法学硕士获得每个轨迹的行为摘要，然后将所有轨迹中的这些汇总起来，以发现重复出现的行为模式，从而产生 15 种规范模式。图 8 突出显示了三种模式。, using an automated behavioral analysis pipeline (Appendix M). We focus on Gemini-3-Flash here, and note that patterns may differ across models. For视觉复杂性visual complexity(a)，较大的型号（Gemini 3 Flash、Kimi-K 2.5）在所有三个级别上实现了大致一致的通过率（例如，Gemini 为 25.3%、20.9%、21.6%）。相比之下，较小的模型表现出急剧下降：Qwen3-VL-2B 从低复杂性软件上的 3.2% 下降到高复杂性软件上的 0.0%。蒸馏提高了各个级别的绝对性能（例如，0.0%(a), larger models (Gemini 3 Flash, Kimi-K 2.5) achieve roughly consistent pass rates across all three levels (e.g., 25.3%, 20.9%, 21.6% for Gemini). In contrast, smaller models show a steep decline: Qwen3-VL-2B drops from 3.2% on low-complexity software to 0.0% on high-complexity software. Distillation improves absolute performance at every level (e.g., 0.0%→高1.3%，3.2%1.3% on high, 3.2%→7.6%（低），但从低到高的下降仍然很陡，这表明视觉复杂性为小模型带来了差异，而仅靠蒸馏无法解决这一问题。7.6% on low), but the decline from low to high remains steep, indicating that visual complexity creates a disparity for small models that distillation alone does not resolve.

对于For领域知识domain knowledge(b)，所有模型都呈现出从通用软件到专用软件的下降趋势，较小的模型表现出更急剧的下降（(b), all models show a downward trend from general to specialized software, with smaller models showing a steeper decline (∼ 3 ×对于我们的 2B 模型：6.4%for our 2B model: 6.4%→2.2%）比大型型号（2.2%) than large models (∼ 1.3 ×双子座：25.6%for Gemini: 25.6%→19.9%）。19.9%).

验证者的稳健性Verifier Robustness我们从两个维度评估验证器的稳健性：a.）它在正确性方面与人类的一致性如何，b.）完整性检查：完整性检查表的频率We evaluate the robustness of the verifier across two dimensions: a.) how well does it agree with humans in terms of correctness, and b.) integrity checks: how often the integrity checklistCint 正确识别快捷方式行为。int correctly identifies shortcut behavior.

基于清单的验证实现了最高的人类一致性。Checklist-based verification achieves highest human agreement.我们在 60 个随机采样的 Gemini-3-Flash 轨迹上比较了三种验证器设计We compare three verifier designs on 60 randomly sampled Gemini-3-Flash trajectories fromCUA-World-测试：（1）基于检查表的 VLM 验证器（第 4.1 节），（2）接收轨迹并输出单个通过/失败判断的直接 VLM 验证器，以及（3）编程验证器，其中模型生成在最终状态上运行并计算分数的脚本。基于清单的验证器与人工注释的任务级一致性达到了 93.3%，而直接 VLM 验证器为 81.7%，编程验证器为 43.3%。每项清单的一致性为 90.9%。编程方法表现不佳主要是因为模型编写了不正确的脚本，无法解析最终状态中存在的数据格式；手动编写的程序验证器可以提供更有力的保证，并且是未来工作的一个有趣的方向。总的来说，我们使用基于清单的 VLM 验证器以及所有实验的特权信息。-Test: (1) our checklistbased VLM verifier (§4.1), (2) a direct VLM verifier that receives the trajectory and outputs a single pass/fail judgment, and (3) programmatic verifiers where a model generates a script that runs on the end state and computes a score. The checklist-based verifier achieves 93.3% task-level agreement with human annotations, compared to 81.7% for the direct VLM verifier and 43.3% for programmatic verifiers. Per-item checklist agreement is 90.9%. The programmatic approach performs poorly primarily because the model writes incorrect scripts that fail to parse the data formats present in the end state; manually authored programmatic verifiers could provide stronger guarantees and are an interesting direction for future work. Overall, we use the checklist-based VLM verifier with privileged information for all experiments.

完整性检查以较低的标记率捕获捷径。Integrity checks catch shortcuts at a low flag rate.跨越Across∼3,000 个 Gemini-3-Flash 轨迹，仅完整性检查表标志3,000 Gemini-3-Flash trajectories, the integrity checklist flags only∼高分跑动的 1.5%（分数1.5% of high-scoring runs (score>75)，总共产生 21 个旗帜，75), producing 21 flags total,

15

其中 15 个为真阳性。我们描述两个有代表性的案例。在法医分析任务中of which 15 are true positives. We describe two representative cases. In a forensic analysis task on尸检Autopsy（数字取证工具），代理遵循正确的工作流程，但在最终报告中伪造了哈希值，而不是复制应用程序中可见的值。在一项统计分析任务中(digital forensics tool), the agent followed the correct workflow but fabricated hash values in its final report rather than copying the values visible in the application. In a statistical analysis task on外延信息Epi Info（流行病学工具包），代理输入参数错误，导致工具显示错误结果，但在其报告中写下了数学上正确的答案，该工具从未显示过该值。在这两种情况下，代理在任务完成方面得分很高，但在完整性检查中却被归零。附录 C.4 中提供了其他示例和详细分类。(epidemiology toolkit), the agent mistyped an input parameter, causing the tool to display an incorrect result, but wrote the mathematically correct answer in its report, a value never shown by the tool. In both cases, the agent scored high on task completion but was zeroed by the integrity check. Additional examples and a detailed breakdown are provided in Appendix C.4.

7.4 Gym-Anything 管道消融7.4 Gym-Anything Pipeline Ablations

训练轨迹长度的影响。Effect of training trajectory length.表 6 比较了在每个软件预算为 25 美元的情况下，每个教师轨迹的前 50 个步骤与所有 200 个步骤的训练。平均而言，这两种设置的表现相似（22.7 与 23.8），但每个软件的模式不同：GIMP 和 Slicer 3D 上的 50 个步骤的训练获胜，而 OpenEMR 上的 200 个步骤的训练更好，可能是因为它的任务需要更长范围的交互。基于此，我们采用两阶段课程进行充分升华：第一轮训练Table 6 compares training on the first 50 vs. all 200 steps of each teacher trajectory under the same $25-per-software budget. On average, the two settings perform similarly (22.7 vs. 23.8), but the per-software pattern differs: training on 50 steps wins on GIMP and Slicer 3D, while training on 200 steps is better on OpenEMR, likely because its tasks require longer-horizon interaction. 提议和放大消融：提议步骤大大提高了放大任务的质量。Propose-and-Amplify Ablation: Proposal step substantially improve amplified task quality.为了评估提议和放大策略（§4），我们使用提议步骤在 10 个软件应用程序中比较了有和没有种子示例生成的任务。我们启动每个生成的任务并使用 VLM 来验证起始状态是否与任务描述匹配。从种子示例放大的任务设置成功率达到 88.9%，而没有种子示例的设置成功率为 55.2%。对三个软件应用程序（Firefox、AstroImageJ、Moodle）的定性分析表明，如果没有种子，该模型默认会演示软件功能，而不是生成现实的专业工作流程，产生较短期限的任务，并编写不太彻底的设置脚本（附录 L）。To evaluate the propose-and-amplify strategy (§4), we compare tasks generated with and without seed examples, using proposal step, across 10 software applications. We launch each generated task and use a VLM to verify whether the starting state matches the task description. Tasks amplified from seed examples achieve an 88.9% setup success rate, compared to 55.2% without seeds. Qualitative analysis on three software applications (Firefox, AstroImageJ, Moodle) reveals that without seeds, the model defaults to demonstrating software features rather than generating realistic professional workflows, produces shorter-horizon tasks, and writes less thorough setup scripts (Appendix L).

跨模型审计：使用单独的 Agentaudit 模型比自我审计捕获更多问题。Cross-Model Auditing: Using a separate model for Agentaudit catches more issues than selfauditing.在§3中，我们认为分离代理In §3, we argued that separating AgentCAgentaudit 消除了自我确认偏差。为了测试这一点，我们比较了同一模型同时充当代理的审计and Agentaudit removes self-confirmation bias. To test this, we compare audits where the same model serves as both AgentCAgentaudit（自审计）针对审计，其中不同的模型充当跨 10 个软件应用程序的 Agentaudit（跨模型审计）。两种配置都会检测所有关键问题，但跨模型审核始终会暴露自我审核遗漏的其他问题。例如，在 OpenELIS 上，自我审计接受真实的患者数据，而跨模型审计则检查播种脚本并将数据识别为硬编码，尽管评论声称来自真实世界的 WHO/CDC 来源。在 10 个软件应用程序中，跨模型审核在每个环境中平均发现 2.1 个额外问题，主要是低到中严重程度。我们在附录 E.5 中提出了三个代表性比较。and Agentaudit (self-audit) against audits where a different model serves as Agentaudit (cross-model audit) across 10 software applications. Both configurations detect all critical issues, but cross-model audits consistently surface additional problems that self-audits miss. For example, on OpenELIS, the self-audit accepts patient data as realistic, while the cross-model audit inspects the seeding script and identifies the data as hardcoded despite comments claiming real-world WHO/CDC sourcing. Across 10 software applications, cross-model audits identify on average 2.1 additional issues per environment, predominantly low-to-moderate severity. We present three representative comparisons in Appendix E.5.

8 相关工作8 Related Work

计算机使用代理的基准。Benchmarks for computer-use agents.先前的计算机使用代理基准要么是静态的，要么是交互式的，但规模较小（表 2；图 5）。静态数据集 [11,38,24,22] 收集数千个情节，但通过动作匹配而不是执行进行评估，从而惩罚有效的替代策略。交互式基准测试提供基于执行的评估，但涵盖了软件领域的一小部分：Web 基准测试 [26,62,23,12,6] 仅限于少数网站，桌面基准测试 [53,7,9,18,55,41,2] 最多跨越少数具有手动编写环境的应用程序，AndroidWorld [37] 涵盖 20 个应用程序。至关重要的是，所有交互式基准都依赖于手动环境创建，限制了其规模，并且没有一个基准可以同时提供训练数据、长期任务或广泛的职业覆盖范围。Prior benchmarks for computer-use agents are either static or interactive but small-scale (Table 2; Figure 5). Static datasets [11, 38, 24, 22] collect thousands of episodes but evaluate via action-matching rather than execution, penalizing valid alternative strategies. Interactive benchmarks provide execution-based evaluation but cover narrow slices of the software landscape: web benchmarks [26, 62, 23, 12, 6] are restricted to a few websites, desktop benchmarks [53, 7, 9, 18, 55, 41, 2] span at most a handful of applications with manually authored environments, and AndroidWorld [37] covers 20 apps. Critically, all interactive benchmarks rely on manual environment creation, limiting their scale, and none simultaneously provides training data, long-horizon tasks, or broad occupational coverage.CUA-World通过自动化环境创建来弥补这些差距，在四个平台上的 200 多个软件应用程序中生成 10K 多个交互式任务，并提供训练/测试分割、长期评估以及基于 GDP 的覆盖所有 22 个 SOC 职业组。addresses these gaps through automated environment creation, yielding 10K+ interactive tasks across 200+ software applications on four platforms, with train/test splits, long-horizon evaluation, and GDP-grounded coverage of all 22 SOC occupation groups.

自动化环境和任务生成。Automated environment and task generation.一些作品在预先存在的环境中生成任务或轨迹[56,40,63,29,35]，但不能创建新的。基于 LLM 的环境生成已被探索用于文本规划 [21]、嵌入式 AI [59]、工具使用 API [50]、代码编辑和 SWE 培训 [31, 64] 以及基于文本的模拟 [60]，但不适用于需要安装、配置和实际数据的真实 GUI 软件。同时，GUI-GENESIS [10] 从单个应用程序生态系统的交互痕迹中合成轻量级 Web 副本，以进行高效的 RL 训练，但不安装或配置真实的软件、处理多操作系统环境或进行长期评估。种子然后放大范式 [49,54,28] 对于大规模生成指令数据是有效的，但目标是文本对而不是可执行环境任务。Several works generate tasks or trajectories within pre-existing environments [56, 40, 63, 29, 35], but cannot create new ones. LLM-based environment generation has been explored for text planning [21], embodied AI [59], tool-use APIs [50], code editing and SWE training [31, 64], and text-based simulations [60], but not for real GUI software requiring installation, configuration, and realistic data. Concurrently, GUI-GENESIS [10] synthesizes lightweight web replicas from interaction traces of a single app ecosystem for efficient RL training, but does not install or configure real software, handle multi-OS environments, or target long-horizon evaluation. The seed-then-amplify paradigm [49, 54, 28] is effective for generating instruction data at scale, but targets text pairs rather than executable environment tasks.Gym-Anything结合了这三者：创建-审核循环，通过由独立审核员验证的编码代理将真实软件转换为交互式环境；提议和放大策略，生成基于实际软件执行的任务；以及共享内存，积累跨环境的知识。combines all three: a creation-audit loop that converts real software into interactive environments via coding agents verified by an independent auditor, a propose-and-amplify strategy that generates tasks grounded in actual software execution, and a shared memory that accumulates learnings across environments.

计算机使用代理的评估。Evaluation of computer-use agents.现有的基准测试主要使用手写的编程验证器来检查最终的系统状态 [53, 62]，这些验证器可靠但劳动密集型，并且仅提供二进制通过/失败。基于VLM的评估已经被探索用于过滤训练轨迹[56]、步骤级轨迹评估[42]和代理轨迹的自主评估[32]，但这些方法缺乏获得真实答案的途径，并且无法检测工作流程捷径。我们基于清单的 VLM 验证器通过合并从环境设置脚本中提取的特权信息来解决这两个问题，无需每个任务代码即可对已知答案进行验证，并包括检测工作流绕过（例如伪造的输出或工具滥用）的完整性检查。Existing benchmarks predominantly use hand-written programmatic verifiers that check the final system state [53, 62], which are reliable but labor-intensive and offer only binary pass/fail. VLM-based evaluation has been explored for filtering training trajectories [56], step-level trajectory assessment [42], and autonomous evaluation of agent trajectories [32], but these approaches lack access to ground-truth answers and cannot detect workflow shortcuts. Our checklistbased VLM verifier addresses both gaps by incorporating privileged information extracted from environment setup scripts, enabling verification against known answers without per-task code, and by including integrity checks that detect workflow bypasses such as fabricated outputs or tool misuse.

我们在附录 N 中提供了扩展的相关工作，其中包括培训方法、经济基础和详细的每个基准的比较。We provide an extended related work with additional coverage of training methods, economic grounding, and detailed per-benchmark comparisons in Appendix N.

9 结论9 Conclusion

在这项工作中，我们介绍了In this work, we introducedGym-Anything，一个可扩展的框架，用于将任意软件转换为交互式计算机使用环境。通过将环境创建减少为设置脚本和配置文件，并将创建本身构建为生成、审核和更正的多代理循环，, a scalable framework for converting arbitrary software into interactive computer-use environments. By reducing environment creation to setup scripts and configuration files, and by framing creation itself as a multi-agent loop of generation, auditing, and correction,Gym-Anything解决了计算机使用代理的一个中心瓶颈：大规模构建现实环境的困难。应用这个框架，我们构建了addresses a central bottleneck in computer-use agents: the difficulty of constructing realistic environments at scale. Applying this framework, we builtCUA-World，基于 GDP 的超过 10K 任务的集合，涵盖跨越不同职业、领域和操作系统的 200 个软件应用程序，以及基于清单的 VLM 验证和训练/测试/长期划分。我们进一步表明, a GDP-grounded collection of over 10K tasks across 200 software applications spanning diverse occupations, domains, and operating systems, together with checklist-based VLM verification and train/test/long-horizon splits. We further showed thatCUA-World提供有用的监督provides useful supervision for

17

通过蒸馏来训练较小的智能体，并且测试时审核可以提高特别是长期任务的性能。同时，training smaller agents through distillation, and that test-time auditing can improve performance on especially long-horizon tasks. At the same time,CUA-World即使对于前沿模型来说，长期也具有挑战性，这表明现实的计算机使用仍然远未解决。-Long is challenging even for frontier models, indicating that realistic computer-use remains far from solved.

更广泛地说，我们的结果表明，计算机使用代理的进步不仅需要更强大的模型，还需要可扩展的方法来构建训练和评估这些模型的环境和任务。我们希望More broadly, our results suggest that progress in computer-use agents will require not only stronger models, but also scalable methods for constructing the environments and tasks on which those models are trained and evaluated. We hope thatGym-Anything , CUA-World，以及发布的代码和基础设施为未来长期、经济相关的计算机使用工作奠定了基础，包括更强大的代理、更强大的验证者以及作为现实世界数字工作基础的软件的更广泛覆盖范围。, and the released code and infrastructure provide a foundation for future work on long-horizon, economically relevant computeruse, including more capable agents, stronger verifiers, and broader coverage of the software that underlies real-world digital work.

11 限制11 Limitations

我们基于 GDP 的软件选择旨在对哪些软件更重要进行合理的排名，而不是精确的美元水平归属。我们使用可访问网络搜索的最强大的法学硕士来估计等式 1 中的份额因子，但其他方法可能会产生更准确的估计。虽然我们专门为无法自由沙盒化（例如，由于许可）的软件选择了最接近的沙盒替代品，但很大一部分专业使用的软件仍然被排除在外，并且免费替代品的性能在多大程度上可以预测其商业对应软件的性能是一个悬而未决的问题。虽然我们手动验证了每个软件环境是否正确启动以及每个Our GDP-grounded software selection is designed to produce a reasonable ranking of which software matters more, not a precise dollar-level attribution. We use the strongest available LLM with websearch access to estimate the share factors in Equation 1, but other methods may yield more accurate estimates. While we specifically select the closest sandboxable alternative for software that cannot be freely sandboxed (e.g., due to licensing), a large fraction of professionally used software remains excluded, and the degree to which performance on free alternatives predicts performance on their commercial counterparts is an open question. While we manually verified that every software environment launches correctly and that everyCUA-World-具有正确起始状态和数据的长任务加载，我们没有端到端地解决所有任务，因此不能保证每个任务都是可解决的。创建完全经过人工验证的基准版本是未来工作的一个有趣方向。最后，我们在整个评估流程中使用 VLM 检查表验证器。手动注释显示出高度的人类一致性，但与任何评估方法一样，VLM 验证器并不完善，并且可能容易受到对抗性利用。开发强大的程序化验证器，每个任务都进行手动审查，将补充当前的方法，并且是另一个有前途的方向。-Long task loads with the correct starting state and data, we did not solve all tasks end-to-end, and therefore cannot guarantee that every task is solvable. Creating a fully human-verified version of the benchmark is an interesting direction for future work. Finally, we use VLM checklist verifiers throughout our evaluation pipeline. Manual annotation shows high human agreement, but like any evaluation method, VLM verifiers are imperfect and may be susceptible to adversarial exploitation. Developing robust programmatic verifiers, each manually vetted per task, would complement the current approach and is another promising direction.

12 道德声明12 Ethics Statement

我们承认，如果自主部署，计算机使用代理可能会带来风险。虽然这项工作引入了环境创建和测试时审核的方法，但它并没有训练或发布超出现有前沿能力的模型。使用的所有软件都是免费提供的，所有数据集都是从公共来源获得或综合生成的。We acknowledge that computer-use agents may pose risks if deployed autonomously. While this work introduces methods for environment creation and test-time auditing, it does not train or release a model that exceeds existing frontier capabilities. All software used is freely available, and all datasets were obtained from public sources or synthetically generated.

基于 GDP 的软件选择：完整的管道A GDP-Grounded Software Selection: Full Pipeline

本附录提供了第 2.2 节中总结的软件选择流程的完整技术细节。This appendix provides the complete technical details for the software selection pipeline summarized in Section 2.2.

A.1 第一阶段：职业GDP计算A.1 Phase 1: Occupation GDP Calculation

我们通过三步缩放程序为 894 个 O*NET 职业中的每一个分配 GDP 值：We assign a GDP value to each of 894 O*NET occupations via a three-step scaling procedure:

  1. 工资单Wage bill：对于每个 SOC-2018 职业，计算就业人数: For each SOC-2018 occupation, compute employment×平均工资来自 BLS OEWS（2024 年 5 月）。mean_wage from BLS OEWS (May 2024).

  2. 劳动报酬Labor compensation：按 BEA 账户的全国比率[Total Com] 工资总额[p][ensation] 调整工资账单。: Scale wage bills by the national ratio[Total Com] Total Wages[p][ensation] from BEA accounts.

  3. 国内生产总值总额Total GDP：按比例调整劳动报酬: Scale labor compensation by

国民生产总值国民薪酬[.]National GDP National Compensation[.]

输出：Output:us_gdp_by_occupation_USD.csv与列：with columns:onetsoc , soc2018 , occupation_title , employment , mean_wage , wage_bill , gdp_labor , gdp_total .

A.2 第 2 阶段：软件发现A.2 Phase 2: Software Discovery

类别提取。Category extraction.职业被打乱并分成 10 组。对于每批，都会提示 LLM (GPT-5)：Occupations are shuffled and batched into groups of 10. For each batch, an LLM (GPT-5) is prompted:“每个职业使用哪些软件类别？”“What software categories does each occupation use?”没有固定的分类法——模型可以自由地发现类别。这产生 5,584 个职业with no fixed taxonomy—the model discovers categories freely. This yields 5,584 occupation→涵盖 894 个职业的类别对。category pairs across 894 occupations.

26

类别重复数据删除。Category deduplication.相似的类别通过精确的标准化名称匹配和模糊字符串相似性（标记排序比率）进行聚类Similar categories are clustered via exact normalized-name matching and fuzzy string similarity (token sort ratio≥92%）。每个簇中最常见的标签被选择作为规范名称。法学硕士（Gemini 3 Flash）裁决不明确的配对。92%). The most frequent label per cluster is selected as the canonical name. An LLM (Gemini 3 Flash) adjudicates ambiguous pairs.

产品枚举。Product enumeration.对于每个独特的类别，法学硕士列举了广泛使用的软件产品（名称、操作系统支持、别名）。通过模糊匹配对每个类别中的产品进行重复数据删除，生成一个目录For each unique category, an LLM enumerates widely-used software products (name, OS support, aliases). Products are deduplicated within each category via fuzzy matching, producing a catalog of∼16,000 种产品16,000 products across∼1,400 个类别。1,400 categories.

A.3 第 3 阶段：目录清理A.3 Phase 3: Catalog Cleanup

三个清理过程可消除 LLM 生成的目录中的噪音：Three cleaning passes remove noise from the LLM-generated catalog:

产品类别验证。Product–category validation.对于每个类别，法学硕士都会验证哪些产品真正属于该类别；不匹配的内容被删除（例如，“电子表格”中的“Photoshop”）。For each category, an LLM verifies which products genuinely belong; mismatches are removed (e.g., “Photoshop” in “Spreadsheets”).

产品存在验证。Product existence verification.所有产品均通过具有 Google 搜索基础的法学硕士进行验证。与真实的当前可用软件不对应的产品（LLM 幻觉）将被删除。All products are verified via an LLM with Google Search grounding. Products that do not correspond to real, currently-available software (LLM hallucinations) are removed.

所有 LLM 调用都以 JSONL 格式缓存，以实现可重复性。All LLM calls are cached in JSONL format for reproducibility.

A.4 第 4 阶段：目录丰富A.4 Phase 4: Catalog Enrichment

三个并行的富集过程对每个产品进行分类：Three parallel enrichment passes classify each product along:

  • 定价Pricing : free | paid | trial | freemium;和; andis_open_source .

  • 接口Interface : gui | cli | both .

  • 可训练性Trainability : sandbox_ready（自由安装）(install freely)| self_hostable（部署在 Docker/VM 中）(deploy in Docker/VM)| free_tier（云免费帐户）(cloud with free account)| restricted（需要付费许可证或组织凭据）。(paid license or org credentials required).

A.5 第 5 阶段：GDP 归属A.5 Phase 5: GDP Attribution

对于每个职业，法学硕士都会收到职业元数据（O*NET 代码、计算机使用重要性/级别分数）、适用的软件类别和可用产品。它返回一个结构化分配：For each occupation, an LLM receives occupation metadata (O*NET code, computer-use importance/level scores), applicable software categories, and available products. It returns a structured allocation:

有约束条件：with constraints:p电脑computer∈ [0 ,1] 以 O*NET 分数为界，类别份额总和为1] bounded by O*NET scores, category shares sum to∼1.0，每个类别内的产品份额总和为 1.0。1.0, and product shares within each category sum to 1.0.

产品 GDP 值是所有职业的总和。按 GDP 排名的热门产品：Microsoft Excel、Microsoft Word、Google Chrome、Microsoft Outlook、Visual Studio Code。重要提示：这些是我们的管道生成的估计值，不应被引用为未来工作中的排名来源。The product GDP values are aggregated across all occupations. Top products by GDP: Microsoft Excel, Microsoft Word, Google Chrome, Microsoft Outlook, Visual Studio Code. Important: These are estimates generated by our pipeline, and should not be cited as source of ranking in future work.

A.6 第 6 阶段：实际准入障碍评估A.6 Phase 6: Practical Access-Barrier Evaluation

定价和接口过滤器是必要的，但还不够。产品可以是免费的、基于 GUI 的，但仍然需要创建外部帐户（Slack、Zoom）、组织凭证（Epic、Slate）或专用硬件（AndroidAPS）。Pricing and interface filters are necessary but not sufficient. A product can be free and GUI-based yet still require external account creation (Slack, Zoom), organizational credentials (Epic, Slate), or specialized hardware (AndroidAPS).

我们批量全部We batch all∼通过以 Google 搜索为基础的法学硕士 (Gemini 3 Pro) 评估 16,600 种产品，评估：16,600 products through an LLM (Gemini 3 Pro) with Google Search grounding, evaluating:

  • 该产品需要外部帐户吗？ （Does the product require an external account? (no | free_optional | free_required | org_required )

  • 是否需要组织/机构凭证？Does it require organizational/institutional credentials?

  • 它需要专门的物理硬件吗？Does it require specialized physical hardware?

  • 总体而言：它可以在沙箱中训练吗？Overall: is it trainable in a sandbox?

我们评估We evaluate the最宽容的most permissive每个产品的版本/模式：如果 NinjaTrader 的免费模拟模式无需登录即可运行，则该产品通过。version/mode of each product: if NinjaTrader’s free simulation mode works without login, the product passes.

27

结果：8,013 个产品 (48%) 可训练； 8,591 人 (52%) 不需要（4,651 人需要组织帐户，3,279 人需要免费帐户，Results: 8,013 products (48%) are trainable; 8,591 (52%) are not (4,651 require organizational accounts, 3,279 require free accounts,∼661 需要硬件）。661 require hardware).

A.7 第七阶段：分级选择A.7 Phase 7: Tiered Selection

一个产品是A product is可选择的selectable如果它满足以下所有条件： (1) 在 Windows、Linux 或 Android 上运行； (2) 并非仅限付费或仅限试用； (3) 不只是 CLI； (4) 沙箱就绪或可自行托管； (5) 通过第 6 阶段的访问障碍评估。if it satisfies all of: (1) runs on Windows, Linux, or Android; (2) not paid-only or trial-only; (3) not CLI-only; (4) sandbox-ready or self-hostable; (5) passes the access-barrier evaluation from Phase 6.

当以其他方式选择不可选择的产品时（例如，GDP 为 $79.5B 的 Bloomberg Terminal），管道会替换由法学硕士排名的同一软件类别中最接近的可选替代品。替代者继承原者的经济地位；原件标记为“已覆盖”。When a non-selectable product would otherwise be chosen (e.g., Bloomberg Terminal at $79.5B GDP), the pipeline substitutes the closest selectable alternative from the same software category, ranked by an LLM. The substitute inherits the original’s economic slot; the original is marked as “covered.”

评选分为五个级别：Selection proceeds across five tiers:

等级Tier 预算Budget 策略Strategy
k1（经济核心）k1 (Economic Core) 100 总体GDP最高的产品Highest GDP products overall

We build environments for 200 based on compute budget. Important Note: Due to bug in our software selection code, we had initially selected 53 environments that would later not come in the 200 selected. However, due to compute constraints, we decided to keep them. However, all of those would have been included in the full 500, which we plan to release sometime in future.

B Gym-Anything 框架：技术细节B Gym-Anything Framework: Technical Details

本附录提供了第 2.3 节中总结的 Gym-Anything 框架的工程细节。该框架的中心设计原则是This appendix provides engineering details for the Gym-Anything framework summarized in Section 2.3. A central design principle of the framework is模块化modularity：规范模式、运行器接口和验证系统的设计都是为了在不修改核心框架的情况下添加新的观察模式、计算后端、操作系统和验证策略。: the specification schema, runner interface, and verification system are all designed so that new observation modalities, compute backends, operating systems, and verification strategies can be added without modifying the core framework.

B.1 环境规范架构B.1 Environment Specification Schema

每个环境都由一个定义Each environment is defined by anenv.json文件包含以下部分：file with the following sections:

接口。Interfaces. observation：模式列表，每种模式都有类型、分辨率和帧速率。目前支持的方式包括: list of modalities, each with type, resolution, and frame rate.
