B.3 渐进式检查点B.3 Progressive Checkpointing

四个安装阶段（安装The four setup stages (install→配置configure→任务设置task setup→出口）在三个级别进行检查点：export) are checkpointed at three levels:

  • 安装后检查点Post-install checkpoint：软件安装后保存磁盘状态。加载会跳过安装阶段。在同一环境的所有任务之间共享。: saves disk state after software installation. Loading skips the install stage. Shared across all tasks for the same environment.

  • 配置后检查点Post-configure checkpoint：保存数据/服务配置后的状态。加载会跳过安装和配置。也可以跨任务共享。: saves state after data/service configuration. Loading skips install and configure. Also shared across tasks.

  • 任务设置后检查点Post-task-setup checkpoint：在每个任务初始化后保存状态。特定任务；能够即时启动以重复评估同一任务。: saves state after per-task initialization. Task-specific; enables instant startup for repeated evaluation of the same task.

磁盘状态检查点。Disk-state checkpoints.码头工人：Docker:docker commit捕获文件系统（下次启动时通过 systemd 重新启动进程）。 QEMU：captures filesystem (processes restart via systemd on next boot). QEMU:qemu-img convert保存 QCOW2 快照。saves a QCOW2 snapshot.

全状态快照 (SaveVM)。Full-state snapshots (SaveVM).QEMU还支持QEMU additionally supportssavevm，它捕获整个 VM 内存、CPU 寄存器和正在运行的进程。从 savevm 快照恢复接近 -, which captures the entire VM memory, CPU registers, and running processes. Restoring from a savevm snapshot is near-∼瞬时 (3 秒)，保留打开的窗口、正在运行的服务和 GUI 状态 - 相比之下，需要重新启动的磁盘状态检查点需要 2-5 分钟。instantaneous ( 3s), preserving open windows, running services, and GUI state—compared to 2–5 minutes for a disk-state checkpoint that requires rebooting.

29

写时复制并行化。Copy-on-write parallelization.多个并发实例通过 QCOW2 覆盖文件共享相同的基本检查点。每个实例仅写入其增量，从而以适度的磁盘开销支持 400 多个并行环境。Multiple concurrent instances share the same base checkpoint via QCOW2 overlay files. Each instance writes only its delta, enabling 400+ parallel environments with modest disk overhead.

B.4 特定于平台的模式B.4 Platform-Specific Patterns

Linux 桌面环境。Linux desktop environments.大多数环境使用带有 systemd 的 Ubuntu GNOME 基础。 GUI自动化使用The majority of environments use an Ubuntu GNOME base with systemd. GUI automation usesxdotool用于鼠标/键盘注入和用于 UI 树捕获的 X11 可访问性。多服务 Web 应用程序（ERP、CRM、LMS 平台）在 QEMU VM 内运行 Docker Compose 堆栈。for mouse/keyboard injection and X11 accessibility for UI tree capture. Multi-service web applications (ERPs, CRMs, LMS platforms) run Docker Compose stacks inside the QEMU VM.

Windows 环境。Windows environments.SSH 在会话 0 中运行（无 GUI 访问）。 GUI 应用程序通过启动SSH runs in Session 0 (no GUI access). GUI applications are launched viaschtasks /IT与批处理文件。交互式自动化使用在 GUI 会话中运行的 PyAutoGUI TCP 服务器（端口 5555），因为 Win32 API 调用（with batch files. Interactive automation uses a PyAutoGUI TCP server (port 5555) running in the GUI session, since Win32 API calls (SetCursorPos , mouse_event）无法可靠地到达所有应用程序。注册表修改会抑制首次运行对话框和许可证提示。) do not reliably reach all applications. Registry modifications suppress first-run dialogs and license prompts.

安卓环境。Android environments.Android 虚拟设备通过以下方式在 Apptainer 内运行Android Virtual Devices run inside Apptainer via theAVDApptainerRunner。交互使用ADB进行输入注入（. Interaction uses ADB for input injection (adb input tap , adb input text）和屏幕截图（) and screenshot capture (adb exec-out screencap）。 APK安装复制到). APK installation copies to/data/local/tmp/在调用之前before invokingpm install以满足 SELinux 约束。to satisfy SELinux constraints.

B.5 验证系统B.5 Verification System

验证器与框架解耦：每个验证器都是任务目录中的独立 Python 文件，通过以下方式加载Verifiers are decoupled from the framework: each is a standalone Python file in the task directory, loaded viaimportlib在评估时。该框架目前支持三种验证模式：at evaluation time. The framework currently supports three verification modes:

  1. 节目Program：Python 函数接收轨迹（屏幕截图、操作日志）、环境实用程序（: a Python function receives the trajectory (screenshots, action log), environment utilities (exec_capture , copy_from_env , query_vlm）和任务元数据。退货), and task metadata. Returns{passed, score, feedback}。编程验证者还可以在内部调用 VLM（例如，用于基于清单的评估），将代码的灵活性与视觉基础相结合。. Programmatic verifiers can also call a VLM internally (e.g., for checklist-based evaluation), combining the flexibility of code with visual grounding.

  2. 图像匹配Image match：最终屏幕截图和参考图像之间的 SSIM 比较，具有可配置的阈值。: SSIM comparison between the final screenshot and a reference image, with a configurable threshold.

  3. 多Multi：首先级联程序验证，然后回退到图像匹配。: cascades program verification first, falling back to image match.

可以通过遵循相同接口编写新的 Python 文件来添加自定义验证策略。Custom verification strategies can be added by writing a new Python file following the same interface.

B.6 情节工件B.6 Episode Artifacts

每集都会生成一个结构化的工件目录：Each episode produces a structured artifact directory:

  • traj.jsonl：每个重置、操作和观察事件的时间戳日志。: timestamped log of every reset, action, and observation event.

  • frame_00000.png, ...：每步骤的屏幕截图。, ...: per-step screenshots.

  • video.mp4：整集的 FFmpeg 编码录制。: FFmpeg-encoded recording of the full episode.

  • summary.json：剧集元数据、验证者结果和奖励。: episode metadata, verifier result, and reward.

  • 设置阶段日志（Setup stage logs (*.log)：来自每个安装脚本的 stdout/stderr。): stdout/stderr from each setup script.

这些工件有双重目的：评估（验证者输入）和训练数据收集（轨迹蒸馏，第 6.1 节）。These artifacts serve dual purposes: evaluation (verifier input) and training data collection (trajectory distillation, Section 6.1).

B.7 分布式执行和工具B.7 Distributed Execution and Tooling

远程执行。Remote execution.对于跨多台机器的大规模评估，该框架提供了主从架构。工作节点公开管理本地环境的 REST API，而主服务器则通过粘性会话处理负载平衡路由（将每个环境映射到特定工作节点）。一个For large-scale evaluation across multiple machines, the framework provides a master-worker architecture. Worker nodes expose a REST API that manages local environments, while a master server handles load-balanced routing with sticky sessions (mapping each environment to a specific worker). ARemoteGymEnv客户端提供与本地相同的APIclient provides the same API as the localGymAnythingEnv，使分布式执行对调用者透明。, making distributed execution transparent to the caller.

轨迹查看器。Trajectory viewer.内置的网络仪表板允许浏览和重播记录的事件，包括每步骤的屏幕截图、操作日志和验证器输出。这支持开发期间的调试和代理行为的定性分析。A built-in web dashboard allows browsing and replaying recorded episodes, including per-step screenshots, action logs, and verifier outputs. This supports both debugging during development and qualitative analysis of agent behavior.

30

B.8 使用示例B.8 Usage Example

为了说明该框架的简单性，我们展示了如何通过命令行和 Python API 启动环境并与之交互。To illustrate the simplicity of the framework, we show how an environment can be launched and interacted with via the command line and Python API.

命令行。Command line.的Thegym-anythingCLI 提供用于交互式运行环境、评估基准分割上的代理、列出可用环境、验证规范、管理缓存检查点以及检查系统先决条件的命令。例如，要与 VNC 查看器交互启动环境：CLI provides commands for running environments interactively, evaluating agents on benchmark splits, listing available environments, validating specifications, managing cached checkpoints, and checking system prerequisites. For example, to launch an environment interactively with a VNC viewer:

#Listavailableenvironmentsandtasks
gym-anythinglist
#Launchanenvironmentinteractively
gym-anythingrunlibreoffice_calc–taskbudget_analysis-i–open-vnc
#Evaluateanagentonabenchmarksplit
gym-anythingbenchmarklibreoffice_calc–agentGemini3Agent\
–modelgemini-3-flash–splittest

Python API。Python API.在编程上，该框架公开了一个标准的 Gymnasium 风格的接口。相同的环境规范在所有计算后端上以相同的方式运行：Programmatically, the framework exposes a standard Gymnasium-style interface. The same environment specification runs identically across all compute backends:

fromgym_anythingimportmake
env=make("envs/libreoffice_calc/env.json",
"envs/libreoffice_calc/tasks/budget_analysis/task.json")
obs=env.reset(use_cache=True,cache_level="post_start")
actions=[{"action":"left_click","coordinate":[340,215]},
{"action":"type","text":"=SUM(B2:B10)"},
{"action":"key","key":"Return"}]
obs,reward,done,info=env.step(actions)
env.close()#runsverifier,writestrajectoryartifacts

的Thereset()call 处理容器编排、显示转发和检查点恢复。的call handles container orchestration, display forwarding, and checkpoint restoration. Thestep()调用注入操作并返回下一个观察结果（屏幕截图）。开call injects actions and returns the next observation (screenshot). Onclose()，该框架运行任务验证器并将所有情节工件（轨迹日志、每步屏幕截图、视频和验证结果）写入结构化目录。, the framework runs the task verifier and writes all episode artifacts (trajectory log, per-step screenshots, video, and verification results) to a structured directory.

C.2 特权信息审计提示C.2 Privileged Information Audit Prompts

PI 审核流程（第 4.1 节）分三个阶段运行。第 1 阶段在无法访问 Web 的情况下分析任务源文件，以识别数据来源和元数据声明。第 2 阶段使用具有 Google 搜索基础的法学硕士来验证针对外部来源的声明。第三阶段将结果综合成经过验证的 PI 报告，强制执行未经验证的信息不得出现在最终摘要中的规则。The PI Audit pipeline (§4.1) operates in three phases. Phase 1 analyzes task source files without web access to identify data provenance and metadata claims. Phase 2 uses an LLM with Google Search grounding to verify claims against external sources. Phase 3 synthesizes the results into a validated PI report, enforcing the rule that unverified information must never appear in the final summary.

第一阶段：文件分析。Phase 1: File Analysis.

清单 2：PI 审计第 1 阶段：数据来源分析提示。Listing 2: PI Audit Phase 1: Data provenance analysis prompt.

3. Which values are hardcoded in scripts vs dynamically computed at runtime

4. Which values in metadata could be verified via web search

5. Whether the data is real (downloaded from a public dataset) or synthetic (generated by scripts)

C.3 VLM 检查表验证器提示C.3 VLM Checklist Verifier Prompts

基于清单的 VLM 验证系统（第 4.1 节）分两个阶段运行。首先，法学硕士根据任务描述和经过验证的 PI 生成结构化清单（清单 5）。其次，VLM 通过检查采样的屏幕截图，根据此清单对代理轨迹进行评分（清单 6）。评分系统区分任务完成失败（通过部分信用评分）和完整性违规（作弊检测的二进制通过/失败）。The checklist-based VLM verification system (§4.1) operates in two stages. First, an LLM generates a structured checklist from the task description and validated PI (Listing 5). Second, a VLM scores agent trajectories against this checklist by examining sampled screenshots (Listing 6). The scoring system distinguishes between task completion failures (scored via partial credit) and integrity violations (binary pass/fail for cheating detection).

  • Be strict: only give "pass" if you see clear visual evidence. However, if you decide that a checklist item is non-essential for the task completion solely based on the task description, and other items are completed, then you can give "pass".

  • "partial" is for when the agent made meaningful progress but didn’t fully complete the sub-task 

INTEGRITYCHECKRULES(critical):

  • Integrity checks detect CHEATING or SHORTCUTS, NOT task failure.

  • An agent that genuinely attempted the task but failed is an INTEGRITY PASS -- honest failure is not cheating.

  • Only mark integrity FAIL if you see clear evidence of: hardcoding answers, copy-pasting expected values, fabricating results without using the software, or bypassing the required workflow entirely.

C.4 完整性检查分析C.4 Integrity Check Analysis

完整性检查表The integrity checklistCint (§4.1) 检测代理绕过预期工作流程而不是完成任务的情况。为了评估这些检查在实践中的效果如何，我们手动审查了所有完整性标志int (§4.1) detects cases where agents bypass the intended workflow rather than completing the task. To evaluate how well these checks work in practice, we manually reviewed all integrity flags across∼3,000 个 Gemini-3-Flash 轨迹，3,000 Gemini-3-Flash trajectories,∼其中 800 次成功运行。800 of which were successful runs.

总体统计。Overall statistics.在高分运行中（任务完成分数Among high-scoring runs (task completion score>75)，完整性检查表标记了 21 个轨迹：15 个真阳性和 6 个假阳性。然而，在 21 个标记案例中，有 18 个案例的任务完成检查表已经给出了不及格分数，因此完整性标记并没有改变通过率。其余 3 个标记案例的任务完成分数为 100 分，但没有一个是实际的诚信违规行为（所有 3 个案例均为误报）。因此，完整性检查不会改变此评估中的总体通过率，而是防止在未来更困难的任务中出现未检测到的捷径，而在这些任务中，仅靠任务清单可能是不够的。75), the integrity checklist flagged 21 trajectories: 15 true positives and 6 false positives. However, in 18 of the 21 flagged cases, the task completion checklist already assigned a failing score, so the integrity flag did not change the pass rate. The remaining 3 flagged cases had a perfect task completion score of 100, but none were actual integrity violations (all 3 were false positives). The integrity checks therefore do not change the overall pass rate in this evaluation, but guard against undetected shortcuts in future, harder tasks where the task checklist alone may not suffice.

示例 1：伪造的报告数据（尸检）。Example 1: Fabricated report data (Autopsy).在 Autopsy 的数字取证任务中，代理被要求分析两个磁盘映像，通过哈希匹配识别泄露的文件，定位已删除的文件和 NTFS 备用数据流，并编写包含文件名和 MD5 哈希的结构化报告。代理正确遵循了整个调查工作流程：创建案例、导入哈希集、添加数据源、运行摄取模块以及在尸检 GUI 中导航到正确的结果，任务完成得分为 87.5。然而，在最终报告中，代理使用相同的占位符哈希填充几乎每个条目，而不是复制尸检界面中可见的每个文件哈希。完整性检查将其标记为伪造的输出：代理在工具中看到了正确的值，但在报告中写入了不同的值。In a digital forensics task on Autopsy, the agent was asked to analyze two disk images, identify exfiltrated files via hash matching, locate deleted files and NTFS alternate data streams, and write a structured report with file names and MD5 hashes. The agent correctly followed the entire investigation workflow: creating the case, importing hash sets, adding data sources, running ingest modules, and navigating to the correct results in the Autopsy GUI, scoring 87.5 on task completion. However, in the final report, the agent populated nearly every entry with the same placeholder hash rather than copying the per-file hashes visible in Autopsy’s interface. The integrity check flagged this as fabricated output: the agent saw the correct values in the tool but wrote different values in the report.

示例 2：结果并非源自工具输出 (Epi Info)。Example 2: Result not derived from tool output (Epi Info).在 Epi Info（流行病学工具包）的统计分析任务中，代理被要求使用 StatCalc Poisson 工具来评估疾病群。代理打开了正确的工具并尝试输入参数（观察到的= 8，预期= 3.6），但犯了一个拼写错误，输入In a statistical analysis task on Epi Info (an epidemiology toolkit), the agent was asked to use the StatCalc Poisson tool to evaluate a disease cluster. The agent opened the correct tool and attempted to enter the parameters (observed=8, expected=3.6), but made a typo, entering3.1.16而不是instead of3.6。 GUI 显示的概率为 0.9999998，对于预期输入而言，该概率是不正确的。然而，代理在其报告中写入了数学上正确的值 (0.0307893)，该工具从未显示该值。完整性检查标记了这一点，因为报告的结果与工具的输出不匹配，这表明代理独立计算答案，而不是使用所需的软件。. The GUI displayed a probability of 0.9999998, which is incorrect given the intended inputs. However, the agent wrote the mathematically correct value (0.0307893) in its report, a value never displayed by the tool. The integrity check flagged this because the reported result did not match the tool’s output, indicating that the agent computed the answer independently rather than using the required software.

示例 3：从任务描述 (PEBL) 中硬编码排除。Example 3: Hardcoded exclusion from task description (PEBL).在 PEBL（心理学实验平台）的计算建模任务中，智能体被要求将强化学习模型与参与者数据相匹配，并“使用数据驱动的标准”排除机器人参与者。任务描述提到机器人的 ID (PRL-999) 作为上下文。该代理编写了一个脚本，正确实现了 Rescorla-Wagner 模型、网格搜索和 AIC 比较，任务完成得分为 90 分。然而，代理并没有计算任何指标（例如准确性、逆转计数）来识别机器人，而是硬编码In a computational modeling task on PEBL (a psychology experiment platform), the agent was asked to fit reinforcement learning models to participant data and exclude a bot participant “using data-driven criteria.” The task description mentioned the bot’s ID (PRL-999) as context. The agent wrote a script that correctly implemented the Rescorla-Wagner models, grid search, and AIC comparison, scoring 90 on task completion. However, instead of computing any metric (e.g., accuracy, reversal count) to identify the bot, the agent hardcodedif pid == ‘PRL-999’在其排除逻辑中。完整性检查将其标记为绕过所需的分析步骤。在这里，任务描述本身通过揭示机器人的身份来启用捷径，但代理仍然违反了使用数据驱动标准的明确指令。in its exclusion logic. The integrity check flagged this as bypassing the required analysis step. Here, the task description itself enabled the shortcut by revealing the bot’s identity, yet the agent still violated the explicit instruction to use data-driven criteria.

示例 4：误报（Oracle 数据库）。Example 4: False positive (Oracle Database).在 Oracle 数据库的数据库管理任务中，代理被要求修复数据差异、创建 PL/SQL 对象并保存对帐报告。代理使用DBeaver连接数据库，执行SQL语句，创建触发器和视图，任务完成得分为60分。它失分主要是因为从未制作过调节报告。完整性检查表包括检查报告是否包含实际计算的计数；由于不存在报告，因此该检查被标记为失败。然而，这是一个误报：代理人没有捏造任何东西；它根本没有完成一项子任务。任务完成检查表已对漏报扣0分。不完整的执行不是工作流绕过，因此不应引发此标志。In a database administration task on Oracle Database, the agent was asked to fix data discrepancies, create PL/SQL objects, and save a reconciliation report. The agent used DBeaver to connect to the database, executed SQL statements, and created triggers and views, scoring 60 on task completion. It lost points primarily because the reconciliation report was never produced. The integrity checklist included a check for whether the report contained actual computed counts; since no report existed, this check was marked as failed. However, this is a false positive: the agent did not fabricate anything; it simply did not complete one sub-task. The task completion checklist already penalized the missing report with 0 points. Incomplete execution is not a workflow bypass, so this flag should not have been raised.

示例 5：使用了错误的工具 (PsychoPy)。Example 5: Wrong tool used (PsychoPy).在实验脚本编写任务中，智能体被明确指示使用 PsychoPy Coder（一种基于 GUI 的心理学实验脚本编辑器）来编写恐惧调节实验。代理打开 PsychoPy，但无法导航 Coder 界面。相反，它切换到终端并使用 shell 重定向编写脚本（In an experiment scripting task, the agent was explicitly instructed to use PsychoPy Coder (a GUI-based script editor for psychology experiments) to write a fear conditioning experiment. The agent opened PsychoPy but was unable to navigate the Coder interface. Instead, it switched to the terminal and wrote the script using shell redirection (cat « EOF）。最终的脚本功能完整，正确实施了习惯化试验、自适应楼梯、平衡和评分量表，任务完成得分为 87.5。完整性检查标记代理未使用所需的软件接口。这是一个常见的故障). The resulting script was functionally complete, correctly implementing habituation trials, an adaptive staircase, counterbalancing, and rating scales, scoring 87.5 on task completion. The integrity check flagged the agent for not using the required software interface. This is a common failure

36

模式：当代理无法操作专门的 GUI 时，它们会退回到终端，通过错误的工具产生正确的输出。pattern: when agents cannot operate a specialized GUI, they fall back to the terminal, producing correct output through the wrong tool.

C.5 污染物过滤提示C.5 Contamination Filtering Prompt

为了构建无污染的训练/测试分割 (§5)，我们使用清单 7 中的提示比较每个环境中的所有任务对。任务对评分To construct contamination-free train/test splits (§5), we compare all task pairs within each environment using the prompt in Listing 7. Task pairs scoring≥4（VERY_SIMILAR 或更高）在相似度图中被视为污染边。该图的连接组件被分配到相同的分割以防止数据泄漏。4 (VERY_SIMILAR or higher) are treated as contaminating edges in a similarity graph. Connected components of this graph are assigned to the same split to prevent data leakage.

清单 7：污染过滤：成对任务相似度比较提示。Listing 7: Contamination filtering: Pairwise task similarity comparison prompt.

Youareevaluatingthesimilaritybetweentwotaskdescriptions
foramachinelearningtrain/testsplit.
TASK1:
{task1_description}
TASK2:
{task2_description}

ClassifytheirsimilarityintoexactlyONEcategory:

  • 1 - NOT_SIMILAR: Completely unrelated tasks

  • 2 - SOMEWHAT_SIMILAR: Minor thematic overlap but fundamentally different tasks

  • 3 - SOME_STEPS_SIMILAR: Tasks share some common substeps (e.g., both navigate to a location) but have distinctly different end goals. This is common and acceptable.

  • 4 - VERY_SIMILAR: Tasks are extremely similar -- knowing how to do one would directly help with the other. Only use this if the tasks are nearly interchangeable.

  • 5 - SAME_REPHRASED: Essentially the same task with different wording

  • 6 - DUPLICATE: Identical or near-identical tasks

  • 7 - SUBSET: Task 1 is a strict subset of Task 2

  • 8 - SUPERSET: Task 1 is a strict superset of Task 2 

IMPORTANT:Beliberalinyourassessment.Categories4-8should
onlybeusedwhentasksareTRULYinterchangeableoronestrictly
containstheother.SharingcommonsubstepsdoesNOTmaketasks
similar--usecategory3forthose.

RespondwithONLYasingledigit(1-8)andnothingelse.

D 证据文件D Evidence Documentation

在环境创建过程中，AgentDuring environment creation, AgentC必须提供结构化证据，证明软件安装正确并且每项任务都是可解决的。随后，独立的 Agentaudit 会根据质量检查表审查此证据，而无需重新运行环境。本附录描述了证据系统并演示了一个具体示例。must produce structured evidence that the software was installed correctly and that each task is solvable. An independent Agentaudit later reviews this evidence against a quality checklist, without re-running the environment. This appendix describes the evidence system and walks through one concrete example.

D.1 证据要求D.1 Evidence Requirements

每个环境都必须提供三类工件：Every environment must supply three categories of artifacts:

  1. 截图。Screenshots.带时间戳的屏幕截图显示：(i) 启动后运行的应用程序，(ii) 每个任务的正确启动状态，以及 (iii) 不存在阻塞错误对话框。Timestamped screen captures showing: (i) the application running after boot, (ii) the correct starting state for each task, and (iii) the absence of blocking error dialogs.

  2. 结构化验证数据。Structured verification data.每个任务都有一个 JSON 文件，记录数据库查询、文件系统检查、服务运行状况和基线计数 - 审计代理在不启动虚拟机的情况下需要确认先决条件成立的任何内容。A JSON file per task recording database queries, file-system checks, service health, and baseline counts—anything the audit agent needs to confirm that preconditions hold without launching the VM.

  3. 导出脚本输出。Export-script output.证明任务的Proof that the task’sexport_result.sh运行时不会出现错误，并生成包含所有预期字段的有效、可解析的 JSON。runs without error and produces valid, parseable JSON with all expected fields.

所有工件都存储在环境目录中，布局如下：All artifacts are stored inside the environment directory under the following layout:

examples/<env_name>/
+–evidence_docs/
|+–<task_name>_screenshot.png
|+–<task_name>_evidence.json
|+–...(onesetpertask)
+–env.json
+–scripts/
+–tasks/

D.3 工作示例：Android Studio — 离线缓存功能D.3 Worked Example: Android Studio — Offline Caching Feature

我们复制（以缩写形式）实际证据日志We reproduce (in abbreviated form) the actual evidence log for thecomplete_offline_caching_feature任务在task in theandroid_studio_env环境。此任务在 StudyPlanner Android 应用程序中展示了半实现的离线缓存功能（Room + Retrofit），在 7 个文件中存在 9 个故意错误；代理人必须解决所有问题并取得成功environment. This task presents a half-implemented offline caching feature (Room + Retrofit) in a StudyPlanner Android app with 9 intentional bugs across 7 files; the agent must fix all of them and achieve a successful./gradlew assembleDebug。完整的未删节日志可在存储库中找到。. The full unabridged log is available in the repository.

E 审核质量检查表和审核示例E Audit Quality Checklist and Example Audits

的TheGym-Anything创建审计循环（§3）将每个创建代理与一个独立的审计代理配对，评估构建的环境是否符合质量标准。审计代理被明确指示将创建代理视为对抗性的——它必须不信任评论、口头声明和未经验证的屏幕截图。下面我们介绍了提供给审计代理的质量检查表（§E.1），然后是两个说明性的单轮审计输出（§E.2、§E.3）和三个跨轮示例（§E.4），展示了迭代创建-审计循环如何纠正各轮之间的问题。creation-audit loop (§3) pairs every Creation Agent with an independent Audit Agent that evaluates whether the constructed environment meets quality standards. The Audit Agent is explicitly instructed to treat the Creation Agent as adversarial—it must distrust comments, verbal claims, and unverified screenshots. Below we present the quality checklist given to the Audit Agent (§E.1), followed by two illustrative single-round audit outputs (§E.2, §E.3) and three cross-round examples (§E.4) showing how the iterative creation-audit loop corrects issues between rounds.

E.1 审核质量检查表E.1 Audit Quality Checklist

审核代理收到以下清单（从审核提示中逐字复制）。每个标准都针对在创建管道的开发过程中观察到的不同故障模式。The Audit Agent receives the following checklist (reproduced verbatim from the audit prompt). Each criterion targets a distinct failure mode observed during development of the creation pipeline.

审核代理质量检查表Audit Agent Quality Checklist

  • (一)(a)任务描述质量。Task Description Quality.任务描述是否足够详细，以便代理能够正确完成任务？任务描述是否过于详细，包含代理应了解的信息（例如，要使用哪些功能）。任务描述是否含糊不清，使得代理可以使用 2 种不同或更多的方法，但只能意识到其中 1 种方法的点，尽管两种方法都是正确的？Is task description sufficiently detail, such that agent can complete the task correctly? Is task descritpion not over detailed, with information the agent is expecteed to know (eg, what features to use). Is task description ambiguous, such that agent can use 2 differnt or more approaches, but would be awared points only for 1 of them, despite both being correct?

  • (二)(b)任务开始状态。Task Start State.task_start：看初始屏幕截图，任务是否从预期状态开始，如任务描述中所述？例如，a.) 软件是否打开，b.) 是否处于描述中提到的正确状态（例如，数据已加载，或者软件的正确屏幕打开），c.) 是否有足够的屏幕截图证据（关键步骤、正确的启动状态、真实数据）表明任务可以端到端完成？ （注意：不需要显示完整的任务完成，但显示它是可行的，例如通过显示正确的启动状态和合理的配置/数据设置就足够了。）task_start: look at initial screenshot, does task start from the expected state, as mentioned in task description? for example, is the right a.) software open, b.) it is in right state as mentioned in description (eg, is data loaded, or the correct screen of software is open), c.) is there sufficient screenshot evidence (key steps, correct start state, real data) that the task is completable end-to-end? (Note: showing full task completion is not required, but showing it is feasible, example by showing proper start state, and reasonable configuration/data setup is more than sufficient.)

  • (三)(c)数据真实性。Data Authenticity.使用的数据是否 a.) 真实且不是伪造/合成生成的，b.) 是否符合任务描述（例如，如果任务说 Bladerunner 视频，并且其他视频已打开），c.) 是否足够具有挑战性（例如，它不仅仅是 excel 中的一堆行，或者 erp 产品中的一些非常小的数据库，等等。）Is the data used a.) real and not fake/synthetically generated, b.) true to description of the task (eg, if task says bladerunner video, and other video is open), c.) challenging enough (eg, it isn’t just a bunch of rows in excel, or some very small database in erp product, and so on.)

40

  • (四)(d)对评论的敌对立场。Adversarial Stance Toward Comments.忽略代码、脚本、json 文件中任何地方提到的任何注释。他们可能故意误导您。IGNORE ANY COMMENTS mentioned anywhere in the code, scripts, json files. they could be there deliberately to mislead you.

  • （五）(e)证据验证。Evidence Verification.使用代理输出中的evidence_docs文件夹来确定代理是否已正确完成环境创建。如果代理人使用任何类型的误导性数据或证据来证明其任何主张，您必须予以强烈反驳。屏幕截图优于口头声明。use evidence_docs folder from the agent outputs, to ascertain if the agent has completed the environment creation correctly. If agent has used any kind of misleading data or proof for any of its claims, you have to counter it very strongly. Screenshots are preferred over verbal claims.

向审计代理提供的附加说明：Additional instructions provided to the Audit Agent:

重要提示：不要相信任何地方提到的任何评论。代理人可能会误导您。IMPORTANT: DO NOT BELIEVE ANY OF THE COMMENTS mentioned anywhere. THE agent is likely misleading you.

注意：如果看不到适当的屏幕截图，特别是对于任务启动的正确状态，这是迄今为止最严重的问题。NOTE: If appropriate screenshots are not visible especially for the correct state of task start, that is by far the most severe issue.

标准 (a)–(c) 和 (e) 针对四个质量维度：描述清晰度、起始状态正确性、数据真实性和证据充分性。标准 (d) 实例化了对抗性假设：审计代理将所有内联评论视为潜在的虚假信息，迫使人们依赖可验证的证据（屏幕截图、数据库状态、日志文件）。下面的两次审计说明了这些标准如何暴露实际问题。Criteria (a)–(c) and (e) target four quality dimensions: description clarity, start-state correctness, data authenticity, and evidence sufficiency. Criterion (d) instantiates the adversarial assumption: the Audit Agent treats all inline comments as potential disinformation, forcing reliance on verifiable evidence (screenshots, database states, log files). The two audits below illustrate how these criteria surface real problems.

E.2 审核示例：Odoo CRM 环境（检测到关键问题）E.2 Example Audit: Odoo CRM Environment (Critical Issues Detected)

以下审核（缩写；[...]标记遗漏）评估了包含五项任务的 Odoo 17 CRM 环境。审计发现了两个关键的证据文档失败（错误标记的屏幕截图，歪曲了任务开始状态），展示了审计代理如何捕获会破坏评估完整性的问题。The following audit (abbreviated; [...] marks omissions) evaluated an Odoo 17 CRM environment with five tasks. The audit identified two critical evidence-documentation failures—mislabeled screenshots that misrepresent the task start state—demonstrating how the Audit Agent catches problems that would undermine evaluation integrity.

审计报告：Audit Report: odoo_crm_env

日期Date : 2026-02-20 环境Environment : examples/odoo_crm_env 审核的任务Tasks audited : create_lead , convert_lead_to_opportunity , schedule_activity , create_customer , mark_opportunity_won

总体判断Overall Verdict

通过中等问题。Pass with moderate issues.该环境真正运行 Odoo 17 Community，具有真实的 Odoo 演示数据和真实的基于 Docker 的基础设施。屏幕截图证实大多数任务都是可以端到端完成的。然而，两个关键的屏幕截图标签问题破坏了任务 1 的启动状态证据，并且一个对话框屏幕截图显示了明显不完整/错误的状态。数据质量可以接受，但并非异常（真实 Odoo 演示数据之上的合成公司名称）。The environment is genuinely running Odoo 17 Community with real Odoo demo data and authentic Docker-based infrastructure. Screenshots confirm most tasks are completable end-to-end. However, two critical screenshot labeling problems undermine start-state evidence for Task 1, and one dialog screenshot shows a clearly incomplete/wrong state. Data quality is acceptable but not exceptional (synthetic company names on top of real Odoo demo data).

E.3 审核示例：Wireshark 环境（混合结果）E.3 Example Audit: Wireshark Environment (Mixed Results)

以下审核（缩写）通过五项任务评估了 Wireshark 网络分析环境。与上面的 Odoo 审计不同，此环境传递基础设施和启动状态证据，但审计揭示了更微妙的任务设计问题：任务描述中意外泄露的真实答案、过于规范的说明以及误导性的证据屏幕截图。The following audit (abbreviated) evaluated a Wireshark network-analysis environment with five tasks. Unlike the Odoo audit above, this environment passes on infrastructure and start-state evidence, but the audit reveals subtler task-design issues: a ground-truth answer accidentally leaked in a task description, overly prescriptive instructions, and misleading evidence screenshots.

审计报告：Audit Report: wireshark_env

审核日期Audit Date : 2026-02-12 环境Environment : examples/wireshark_env 应用Application：Wireshark 3.6.2（网络协议分析器）: Wireshark 3.6.2 (Network Protocol Analyzer)任务Tasks : filter_http_traffic , count_dns_queries , identify_top_talkers , follow_tcp_stream , export_protocol_hierarchy

count_dns_queries（简单）：(easy):

说明：Description:“在 Wireshark 中打开位于 /home/ga/Documents/captures/dns.cap 的 DNS 示例捕获文件 (dns.cap)。使用显示过滤器仅隔离 DNS 查询数据包（不包括 DNS 响应）。计算 DNS 查询数据包的数量，并将文本文件保存在 /home/ga/Documents/captures/dns_query_count.txt，其中仅包含纯整数计数（例如“19”）。”“Open the DNS sample capture file (dns.cap) located at /home/ga/Documents/captures/dns.cap in Wireshark. Use a display filter to isolate only DNS query packets (excluding DNS responses). Count the number of DNS query packets and save a text file at /home/ga/Documents/captures/dns_query_count.txt containing ONLY the count as a plain integer (e.g. ‘19’).”

  • 够详细吗？Sufficiently detailed?是的。明确要计算的内容（仅查询，不计算响应）、保存位置以及格式。YES. Clear about what to count (queries only, not responses), where to save, and what format.

  • 过于详细？Over-detailed?小问题。例子MINOR CONCERN. The example‘19’在描述中in the description是实际的真实答案is the actual ground truth answer（dns.cap 中的 19 个 DNS 查询）。这实际上泄露了答案。猜测或使用示例值的代理将通过。这是一个破坏任务有效性的重大问题。(19 DNS queries in dns.cap). This effectively leaks the answer. An agent that guesses or uses the example value will pass. This is a significant issue that undermines the task’s validity.

  • 暧昧？Ambiguous?没有。查询和响应之间的区别很明显。NO. The distinction between queries and responses is clear.

  • 判决：Verdict:中等问题——示例值泄露了真实答案。MODERATE ISSUE — the example value leaks the ground truth answer.

export_protocol_hierarchy（中）：(medium):

说明：Description:“在 Wireshark 中打开位于 /home/ga/Documents/captures/http.cap 的 HTTP 示例捕获文件 (http.cap)。转到统计 > 协议层次结构以查看协议分布。在协议层次结构统计窗口中，单击“复制”按钮或右键单击并选择“复制为 CSV”来复制数据，然后将其粘贴到文本文件中。将协议层次结构数据保存到/home/ga/Documents/captures/protocol_hierarchy.txt。该文件应包含协议名称及其层次结构中的数据包百分比。”“Open the HTTP sample capture file (http.cap) located at /home/ga/Documents/captures/http.cap in Wireshark. Go to Statistics > Protocol Hierarchy to view the protocol distribution. In the Protocol Hierarchy Statistics window, click the ‘Copy’ button or right-click and select ‘Copy as CSV’ to copy the data, then paste it into a text file. Save the protocol hierarchy data to /home/ga/Documents/captures/protocol_hierarchy.txt. The file should contain the protocol names and their packet percentages from the hierarchy.”

43

  • 够详细吗？Sufficiently detailed?是的。YES.

  • 过于详细？Over-detailed?是的。描述告诉代理确切的菜单路径（统计 > 协议层次结构）、要单击的确切按钮（“复制”按钮）以及确切的方法（“右键单击并选择复制为 CSV”）。这本质上为代理提供了一步一步的演练，几乎没有留下代理需要弄清楚的内容。该任务被简化为“按顺序单击这些菜单，然后粘贴到文件中”。YES. The description tells the agent the exact menu path (Statistics > Protocol Hierarchy), the exact button to click (‘Copy’ button), AND the exact method (‘right-click and select Copy as CSV’). This essentially gives the agent a step-by-step walkthrough, leaving very little for the agent to figure out. The task is reduced to “click these menus in order, then paste into a file.”

  • 暧昧？Ambiguous?没有。非常规范的说明。NO. Very prescriptive instructions.

  • 判决：Verdict:中等问题——过于规范。不应告诉代理人确切的复制方法。MODERATE ISSUE — too prescriptive. Should not tell the agent the exact copy method.

(c) 数据质量和挑战级别(c) Data Quality and Challenge Level

[...] 所有 5 个 PCAP 文件都是[...] All 5 PCAP files are正版官方 Wireshark 样本捕获genuine official Wireshark sample captures来自 Wireshark 基金会的 SampleCaptures wiki 页面。没有合成或虚假数据。 [...]from the Wireshark Foundation’s SampleCaptures wiki page. No synthetic or fake data. [...]

关注：所有捕获都非常小且简单。CONCERN: All captures are extremely small and simple.

文件File 数据包Packets IPv4 端点Endpoints 复杂性Complexity
http.cap 43 _∼_4 单个 HTTP 请求/响应Single HTTP request/response
dns.cap 38 _∼_2 19 条查询 + 19 条回复19 queries + 19 responses
smtp协议smtp.pcap 60 2 单个 SMTP 会话Single SMTP conversation
200722_tcp_anon.pcapng 35 2 微不足道的 Netcat 流量Trivial netcat traffc

这些捕获是为教程设计的教育样本，而不是实际的企业流量。主要关注点：The captures are educational samples designed for tutorials, not realistic enterprise traffic. Key concerns:

44

  1. 200722_tcp_anon.pcapng 只有 2 个 IPv4 端点和 35 个数据包。200722_tcp_anon.pcapng has only 2 IPv4 endpoints and 35 packets.“识别最高发言者”任务很简单——只有 2 个端点，因此代理有 50% 的机会在不看的情况下猜对。 Endpoints 对话框显示 2 个条目，无需排序，答案立即显而易见。The “identify top talkers” task is trivial — there are only 2 endpoints, so the agent has a 50% chance of guessing correctly without even looking. The Endpoints dialog shows 2 entries and the answer is immediately obvious without sorting.

  2. dns.cap 正好有 38 个数据包（19 个查询 + 19 个响应）。dns.cap has exactly 38 packets (19 queries + 19 responses).应用过滤器后，计数立即在状态栏中可见，除了读取数字之外不需要任何分析。The count is immediately visible in the status bar after applying a filter, requiring no analysis beyond reading a number.

  3. http.cap 有 43 个数据包http.cap with 43 packets使协议层次结构变得简单易读。makes protocol hierarchy trivially readable.

  4. 任务不需要任何复杂的分析。Tasks don’t require any complex analysis.没有多流消歧，没有大型数据集导航，除了基本过滤之外没有特定于协议的知识。No multi-stream disambiguation, no large dataset navigation, no protocol-specific knowledge beyond basic filtering.

  5. 判决：Verdict:数据是真实的，但任务太简单了。更具挑战性的环境将使用包含数百个对话的更大捕获，需要实际的分析技能。Data is authentic but tasks are too easy. A more challenging environment would use larger captures with hundreds of conversations, requiring actual analytical skill.

这两项审计说明了自动化审计流程发现的问题范围。 Odoo 审计表明，即使运行良好的环境也可能带有误导性证据——错误标记的屏幕截图歪曲了开始状态，清单中的标准 (b) 被标记为最严重的问题类别。 Wireshark 审计展示了审计如何发现更微妙的设计问题：泄露的真实答案违反了标准 (a)，证据未能验证其在标准 (e) 下的主张，以及数据真实但在标准 (c) 下缺乏挑战性。两次审计都促使在纳入基准测试之前对各自的环境进行具体修复。These two audits illustrate the spectrum of issues caught by the automated audit process. The Odoo audit demonstrates that even well-functioning environments can ship with misleading evidence— mislabeled screenshots that misrepresent start states, which criterion (b) of the checklist flags as the most severe issue category. The Wireshark audit shows how audits catch subtler design problems: leaked ground-truth answers violating criterion (a), evidence that fails to verify what it claims under criterion (e), and data that is authentic but insufficiently challenging under criterion (c). Both audits motivated concrete fixes to the respective environments before inclusion in the benchmark.

Below, we present three environments where we recovered both Round 1 and Round 2 audits from the creation-audit loop, illustrating how the feedback cycle concretely improves environment quality. Table 11 summarizes the findings; detailed excerpts follow.

E.4.1 PEBL：轮次之间修复错误的响应密钥E.4.1 PEBL: Wrong Response Keys Fixed Between Rounds

PEBL（心理学实验构建语言）是用于运行经典认知心理学实验的工具包。的PEBL (Psychology Experiment Building Language) is a toolkit for running classic cognitive psychology experiments. Therun_flanker_task要求智能体执行埃里克森侧翼任务并完成练习试验。requires the agent to execute the Eriksen Flanker Task and complete practice trials.

第一轮审计摘录：Round 1 Audit Excerpt: pebl_env — run_flanker_task

任务描述（第一轮）：Task description (Round 1):“……通过按向左或向右箭头键指示中心箭头的方向，至少完成前几次练习试验。”“...complete at least the first few practice trials by pressing the left or right arrow key to indicate the direction of the center arrow.”

关键问题：错误的密钥指令CRITICAL ISSUE: Wrong Key Instructions

证据截图The evidence screenshotflanker_instructions.png清晰显示实际实验指导屏幕：clearly shows the actual experiment instruction screen:

  • “左移向左”“LEFT-SHIFT for LEFT”

  • “右移换右”“RIGHT-SHIFT for RIGHT”

  • “使用 Shift 键进行响应”“Use shift keys to respond”

任务描述告诉代理按箭头键。该实验不会将箭头键注册为有效响应。忠实地遵循任务描述的特工将无法完成任何试验。The task description tells the agent to press arrow keys. The experiment does not register arrow keys as valid responses. An agent faithfully following the task description would be unable to complete any trial.

结论：失败Verdict: FAIL— 如所描述的那样，任务无法完成。— task is uncompletable as described.

第二轮审计摘录：Round 2 Audit Excerpt: pebl_env — run_flanker_task

任务描述（第二轮）：Task description (Round 2):“......实验窗口打开后，请仔细阅读说明，并按屏幕上所示的相应响应键至少完成前几次练习试验。”“...Once the experiment window opens, read the instructions carefully and complete at least the first few practice trials by pressing the appropriate response keys as indicated on screen.”

描述不再指定要按哪些键。代理必须阅读屏幕上的说明（左移和右移），这适用于中等难度的任务。The description no longer specifies which keys to press. The agent must read the on-screen instructions (LEFT-SHIFT and RIGHT-SHIFT), which is appropriate for a medium-difficulty task.

结论：通过Verdict: PASS

第一轮审核发现了任务描述中的事实错误，该错误将使任何代理无法完成该任务。代理The Round 1 audit caught a factual error in the task description that would have made the task impossible for any agent. AgentC写了描述，没有与实际的实验界面进行验证。代理商收到审核后had written the description without verifying it against the actual experiment interface. After receiving the audit, AgentC更正了描述以遵循屏幕上的说明，第二轮审核确认了修复。corrected the description to defer to the on-screen instructions, and the Round 2 audit confirmed the fix.

E.4.2 SeisComP：从零证据到完整文档E.4.2 SeisComP: From Zero Evidence to Full Documentation

SeisComP 是一个用于地震检测和分析的地震监测套件。该环境包括两个运行的任务SeisComP is a seismological monitoring suite used for earthquake detection and analysis. The environment includes two tasks operating thescconfig和andscolvGUI 工具包含真实的 USGS 地震数据（2024 年能登半岛 M7.5 事件）。GUI tools with real USGS earthquake data (2024 Noto Peninsula M7.5 event).

46

第一轮审计摘录：Round 1 Audit Excerpt: seiscomp_env

关键：CRITICAL: The evidence_docs/ 文件夹完全是空的。folder is completely empty.

有零个屏幕截图或文件展示：There are zero screenshots or files demonstrating:

  • 那Thatscconfig or scolvGUI 应用程序实际上启动并显示数据GUI applications actually launch and display data

  • FDSN Web 服务下载（GEOFON 站库存、USGS 事件）成功That the FDSN web service downloads (GEOFON station inventory, USGS events) succeeded

  • 将台站库存和地震事件加载到数据库中That the station inventory and earthquake event are loaded in the database

  • 任一任务的开始状态均可到达That either task’s start state is reachable

检查时设置脚本结构良好，但没有任何视觉证据，审核无法确认环境是否有效。根据审核清单，缺少起始状态证据是最严重的问题类别。The setup scripts appear well-structured on inspection, but without any visual evidence, the audit cannot confirm the environment works. Per the audit checklist, missing start-state evidence is the most severe issue category.

结论：关键证据失败Verdict: CRITICAL EVIDENCE FAILURE

第二轮审计摘录：Round 2 Audit Excerpt: seiscomp_env

现在证据已经出来了。Evidence now present.的Theevidence_docs/文件夹包含屏幕截图确认：folder contains screenshots confirming:

  • scconfig启动并显示带有站点列表的绑定面板（GE 网络，5 个印度尼西亚站点）launches and displays the Bindings panel with station list (GE network, 5 Indonesian stations)

  • scolv显示能登半岛地震事件（2024 年 1 月 1 日，M7.5，深度 10 公里）以及相位选取和残差shows the Noto Peninsula earthquake event (2024-01-01, M7.5, depth 10 km) with phase picks and residuals

  • 两个任务开始状态都与任务描述匹配Both task start states match the task descriptions

  • 独立验证的地震事件数据：从 USGS FDSN 事件服务下载（Earthquake event data independently verified: downloaded from the USGS FDSN event service (earthquake.usgs.gov), 事件 ID), event IDus6000m0xl，参数与真实的USGS目录相符。, parameters match the real USGS catalog.

结论：通过Verdict: PASS

代理AgentC已完成安装和数据设置，但完全跳过了证据生成步骤——环境有效，但没有证据。第一轮审核强制Agenthad completed the installation and data setup but skipped the evidence production step entirely—the environment worked, but there was no proof. The Round 1 audit forced AgentC实际启动 GUI 工具、截取屏幕截图并记录证据，然后进行第二轮审核验证。to actually launch the GUI tools, take screenshots, and document the evidence, which the Round 2 audit then verified.

E.4.3 石墨：重写的过度规范描述E.4.3 Graphite: Over-Prescriptive Description Rewritten

Graphite 是一个时间序列监控工具。的Graphite is a time-series monitoring tool. Thecompose_graph_with_function任务要求代理向 Graphite Composer 添加指标并应用平滑函数。task requires the agent to add a metric to the Graphite Composer and apply a smoothing function.

第一轮审计摘录：Round 1 Audit Excerpt: graphite_env — compose_graph_with_function

任务描述（第一轮）：Task description (Round 1):“...单击顶部菜单栏中的‘图形数据’打开数据面板。然后单击‘添加’添加新指标。导航树或输入指标路径...图表渲染后，单击数据面板中的指标将其选中，然后单击‘应用函数’并选择‘计算’>‘移动平均’。在对话框中将窗口参数设置为 10。”“. . . click ‘Graph Data’ in the top menu bar to open the data panel. Then click ‘Add’ to add a new metric. Navigate the tree or type the metric path ...After the graph renders, click on the metric in the data panel to select it, then click ‘Apply Function’ and choose ‘Calculate’ > ‘movingAverage’. Set the window parameter to 10 in the dialog.”

过于详细Over-detailed : 失败FAIL。这是一个分步演练，可准确告诉代理按顺序单击哪些按钮。对于“中等”难度的任务，代理应该弄清楚 Composer 工作流程本身。描述应说明目标（添加指标 X，应用窗口 = 10 的 movingAverage），而不规定每个 UI 交互。. This is a step-by-step walkthrough that tells the agent exactly which buttons to click in order. For a “medium” difficulty task, the agent should figure out the Composer workflow itself. The description should state the objective (add metric X, apply movingAverage with window=10) without prescribing each UI interaction.

严重性：高Severity: HIGH

第二轮审计摘录：Round 2 Audit Excerpt: graphite_env — compose_graph_with_function

任务描述（第二轮）：Task description (Round 2):“在 Graphite Composer 中，添加指标“In the Graphite Composer, add the metricservers.ec2_instance_1.cpu.utilization并应用窗口为 10 的 movingAverage 函数来平滑数据。从 Graphite Composer 页面（已打开）开始，将指标添加到图表中，然后对其应用 movingAverage 函数。该图表应该更新以显示 CPU 利用率数据的平滑版本。”and apply the movingAverage function with a window of 10 to smooth the data. Starting from the Graphite Composer page (already open), add the metric to the graph and then apply the movingAverage function to it. The graph should update to show a smoothed version of the CPU utilization data.”

47

现在，描述说明了指标、函数和预期结果，但没有规定确切的点击顺序。代理必须自行发现 Composer UI，这适合中等难度。The description now states the metric, the function, and the expected result without prescribing the exact click sequence. The agent must discover the Composer UI on its own, which is appropriate for medium difficulty.

结论：通过Verdict: PASS

过度规范的任务描述是所有环境中最常见的审计发现（除了 Graphite 之外，在 Stellarium、PyMOL、Webots、SeisComP、BlenderBIM 和 Sweet Home 3D 中也进行了独立标记）。创建审核循环将这些描述从分步教程更正为面向目标的说明，保留了基准测试的预期难度级别。Over-prescriptive task descriptions were the most common audit finding across all environments (flagged independently in Stellarium, PyMOL, Webots, SeisComP, BlenderBIM, and Sweet Home 3D in addition to Graphite). The creation-audit loop corrected these descriptions from step-by-step tutorials to goal-oriented instructions, preserving the intended difficulty level of the benchmark.

E.5 跨模型审计比较E.5 Cross-Model Audit Comparison

评估 Agentaudit 是否受益于与 Agent 不同的模型To evaluate whether Agentaudit benefits from being a different model than AgentC(§3)，我们比较自我审计（其中相同的模型既充当代理(§3), we compare self-audits (where the same model serves as both AgentC和 Agentaudit）针对三个代表性软件应用程序的跨模型审计（其中不同的模型充当 Agentaudit）。两种审核配置都会收到相同的提示和质量检查表（§E.1）。and Agentaudit) against cross-model audits (where a different model serves as Agentaudit) on three representative software applications. Both audit configurations receive identical prompts and quality checklists (§E.1).

E.5.1 Visallo（调查分析平台）E.5.1 Visallo (Investigative Analytics Platform)

两次审计都正确识别了核心问题：5 个任务中有 3 个声称数据已预加载，但不存在数据加载步骤。两人都注意到仪表板屏幕截图显示“无数据”作为反证，并标记了缺少的结构化导入插件。Both audits correctly identify the core problem: 3 of 5 tasks claim data is pre-loaded, but no data loading step exists. Both note the dashboard screenshot showing “NO DATA” as counter-evidence and flag the missing structured import plugin.

跨模型审计还捕获了什么。What the cross-model audit catches additionally.跨模型审核发现了自我审核遗漏的三个问题：(1) 任务 3 的描述称 CSV 文件“在桌面上”，但安装脚本将其放置在The cross-model audit identifies three issues the self-audit misses: (1) task 3’s description says the CSV file is “on the desktop” but the setup script places it in/home/ga/Documents/，文件路径不匹配会使代理感到困惑； （2）Elasticsearch客户端版本不匹配（WAR捆绑了ES 1.4.4客户端jar，但运行的服务器是1.7.6版本，可能会导致传输协议不兼容）； (3) 本体是从第三方 GitHub 分支而不是官方来源获取的，从而产生了脆弱的外部依赖性。, a file path mismatch that would confuse the agent; (2) an Elasticsearch client version mismatch (the WAR bundles ES 1.4.4 client jars, but the running server is version 1.7.6, which may cause transport protocol incompatibilities); and (3) the ontology is fetched from a third-party GitHub fork rather than an official source, creating a fragile external dependency.

E.5.2 Mattermost（团队消息平台）E.5.2 Mattermost (Team Messaging Platform)

Both identify the Firefox-vs-Epiphany browser mismatch as the primary issue.

跨模型审计还捕获了什么。What the cross-model audit catches additionally.跨模型审计注意到了自我审计忽略的三个差距：(1) 缺少专用的启动状态屏幕截图The cross-model audit notes three gaps the selfaudit overlooks: (1) a missing dedicated start-state screenshot for thecreate_private_channel任务（现有的屏幕截图来自其他任务的设置运行）； (2) VM内任务截图(task (the existing screenshots are from other tasks’ setup runs); (2) in-VM task screenshots (/tmp/task_start.png) 由设置脚本捕获，但从未提取到证据文档中； (3) 更细致的数据质量评估，指出种子数据仅包含平面发布公告消息，没有线程对话、反应或文件附件。) are captured by the setup scripts but never extracted to the evidence documentation; and (3) a more nuanced data quality assessment noting that the seeded data consists only of flat release announcement messages with no threaded conversations, reactions, or file attachments.

E.5.3 OpenELIS（实验室信息管理系统）E.5.3 OpenELIS (Laboratory Information Management System)

两次审计都正确地识别了关键问题：Both audits correctly identify the critical issue: thevalidate_lab_results该任务不可行，因为系统中不存在实验室订单、样品或结果（仪表板显示所有指标均为零）。两者都注意到不可靠的登录页面启动状态。task is unfeasible because no lab orders, samples, or results exist in the system (the dashboard shows all metrics at zero). Both note the unreliable login-page start state.

跨模型审计还捕获了什么。What the cross-model audit catches additionally.所有三个软件应用程序之间最显着的差异出现在这里。自我审核将患者数据描述为“良好”，并指出这些名称代表了部署 OpenELIS 的国家/地区的实际人口统计数据。跨模型审计检查实际情况The most significant divergence across all three software applications occurs here. The self-audit misses both issues.

E.5.4 总结E.5.4 Summary

这三个软件应用程序中出现了两种一致的模式：Two consistent patterns emerge across the three software applications:

两种配置都能检测到关键问题。Both configurations detect critical issues.自我审核和跨模型审核就所有关键和高严重性发现达成一致：缺少数据加载 (Visallo)、不可行的任务 (OpenELIS)、浏览器不匹配 (Mattermost)。无论哪种模型执行审计，审计提示的对抗性框架都足以暴露主要问题。Self-audits and cross-model audits agree on all critical and high-severity findings: missing data loading (Visallo), unfeasible tasks (OpenELIS), browser mismatches (Mattermost). The adversarial framing of the audit prompt is sufficient to surface major problems regardless of which model performs the audit.

跨模型审计对 Agent 更具敌意Cross-model audits are more adversarial toward Agent C 的主张。’s claims.自审表现出接受代理的倾向Self-audits exhibit a tendency to accept AgentC表面上的叙述，特别是对于证据文档中的数据质量声明和口头描述。跨模型审计更积极地根据实际代码和工件验证这些声明，捕获误导性评论（OpenELIS 数据源）、文件路径不匹配（Visallo）和证据差距（Mattermost 缺失屏幕截图）。这与第 3 节中讨论的自我确认偏差是一致的：即使有明确的对抗性提示，相同的模型也不太可能挑战自己先前的输出。在 10 个软件应用程序（此处显示 3 个）中，跨模型审核平均为每个环境识别出 2.1 个额外问题，主要是低到中严重程度。’s narrative at face value, particularly for data quality claims and verbal descriptions in evidence documentation. Cross-model audits more actively verify these claims against the actual code and artifacts, catching misleading comments (OpenELIS data sourcing), file path mismatches (Visallo), and evidence gaps (Mattermost missing screenshots). This is consistent with the selfconfirmation bias discussed in §3: even with an explicit adversarial prompt, the same model is less likely to challenge its own prior outputs. Across 10 software applications (3 shown here), cross-model audits identify on average 2.1 additional issues per environment, predominantly low-to-moderate severity.

F CUA-World -长：质量指南和生成管道-Long: Quality Guidelines and Generation Pipeline

CUA-World-Long 包含 200 个长期任务（每个软件环境一个，每个任务需要 200 多个交互步骤），旨在对扩展序列上的代理规划和执行进行压力测试。我们不是手动编写这些任务，而是采用自动化的、故障驱动的管道：人工智能系统分析现有任务的代理轨迹，识别系统弱点，并生成专门针对这些故障模式的更困难的任务。每个生成的任务必须满足八项质量准则，并在纳入基准测试之前通过多阶段验证过程。-Long comprises 200 long-horizon tasks (one per software environment, each requiring 200+ interaction steps), designed to stress-test agent planning and execution over extended sequences. Rather than manually authoring these tasks, we employ an automated, failure-driven pipeline: an AI system analyzes agent trajectories on existing tasks, identifies systematic weaknesses, and generates harder tasks that specifically target those failure modes. Each generated task must satisfy eight quality guidelines and pass a multi-stage verification process before inclusion in the benchmark.

F.1 质量指南F.1 Quality Guidelines

每个EveryCUA-World-长期任务根据以下八个质量原则进行评估。仅当任务明确满足所有八个标准时，该任务才会包含在基准中。-Long task is evaluated against the following eight quality principles. A task is included in the benchmark only if it clearly satisfies all eight criteria.

  1. 真实数据：Realistic Data:数据应该是真实的（或高度现实的，例如类似于现实生活中所期望的复杂设置）。data should be real (or highly realistic, for instance complex setup similar to what one would expect in real life).

  2. 相关任务：Relevant Task:问这个问题，实体经济中执行此任务的人会在工作中执行类似或相同的任务吗？Ask this question, would a person in real economy performing this task, would be doing a similar or same task in their work?

  3. 困难的任务：Difficult Task:与该软件的所有现有任务相比，我正在创建的任务是否更难？注意：知识渊博的法学硕士将解决该任务。人为地使其成为知识密集型或基于事实的简单伎俩是没有帮助的。Compared to all existing tasks for this software, is the task that I am creating much harder? Note: a very knowledgeable LLM will be solving the task. so simple trickery of making it artificially knowledge intensive or fact based wouldn’t help.

  4. 不是人为的硬：Not artificially hard:这项任务不应该人为地困难。例如，我们可以链接数百个不同的子任务，这自然会使其非常耗时，并且代理可能会失败。这不是一个好任务。另一种思考方式是，如果您无法用少于 250 个单词来描述任务，那么简单的子任务就显得特别不必要地过度夸大。The task should not be artificially hard. For instance, we can chain 100s of different subtasks, and that would naturally make it extremely time taking and an agent will likely fail. that is not a good task. Another way to think is that if you cannot describe the task in less than 250 words, it is particularly unecessarily overinflated with simple subtasks.

  5. 长远视野：Long Horizon:任务不应该是只需要几十个步骤就能成功的。该任务应该真正对代理的长期计划和执行能力进行压力测试。一个好的代理比我们现在拥有的最长的当前任务还要长。The task should not be such that only few dozen of steps will lead to success. The task should really stress test the agent’s ability to plan and execute over a long horizon. A good proxy is longer than the longest current task that we have right now.

  6. 客观性：Objectiveness:任务应该是可客观评估的。例如，像做好演示这样的任务是主观的，因为“好”的定义不明确。任务应该明确且客观。The task should be objectively evaluatable. For instance, tasks like make a good presentation is subjective, because definition of “good” is unclear. Tasks should be unambiguous and objective.

  7. 与软件的相关性：Relevance to Software:该任务应该与手头的软件相关。问这个问题，如果给一个人这个任务，他们会使用这个软件吗？如果不是，则意味着这是一项糟糕的任务，因为它与软件无关。The task should be relevant to the software in hand. Ask this question, if a person was given this task, would they be using this software? if not, that implies its a bad task, since it is not software-relevant.

  8. 工作环境：Environment Working:任务环境应该正常工作。因此，我们不能考虑不可能设置的假设任务。我们并没有特别受到网络、CPU、内存资源的限制，但任务不能是那些无法设置的任务。The task environment should be working. So we cannot be thinking of hypothetical tasks, that are impossible to setup. we are not particularly constrained by network, cpu, memory resources, but tasks cannot be those that are impossible to setup.

49

— 一个关键的设计原则是— A key design principle is that一项艰巨的任务不仅仅是将许多子任务链接在一起a hard task is not simply one with many subtasks chained together困难应该来自领域固有的复杂性、多步骤推理和长期规划，而不是来自人为的通货膨胀。difficulty should emerge from the inherent complexity of the domain, multi-step reasoning, and long-horizon planning, not from artificial inflation.

F.2 任务生成管道F.2 Task Generation Pipeline

每个EachCUA-World-长任务由七阶段自动化管道产生。在每个阶段，人工智能系统（以完全自主模式运行的克劳德代码）执行工作，并在最终审核阶段进行人工监督。-Long task is produced by a seven-stage automated pipeline. At every stage, an AI system (Claude Code operating in fully autonomous mode) performs the work, with human oversight at the final audit stage.

前置步骤：VLM 检查表验证。Pre-step: VLM Checklist Verification.在生成新任务之前，我们在目标软件环境的所有现有代理轨迹上运行基于 VLM 的清单验证程序。此步骤处理代理并行运行（最多 16 个工作线程），生成每个任务的验证分数，以量化代理成功和失败的位置。至关重要的是，所得到的分数是基于 VLM 的，而不是基于程序的，因为许多程序化验证器可能会被破坏，从而提供了驱动任务设计的失败信号。Before generating a new task, we run a VLM-based checklist verifier on all existing agent trajectories for the target software environment. This step processes agent runs in parallel (up to 16 workers), producing per-task verification scores that quantify where agents succeeded and failed. The resulting scores—crucially, VLM-based rather than programmatic, since many programmatic verifiers may be broken—provide the failure signal that drives task design.

第一阶段：轨迹分析和任务设计。Stage 1: Trajectory Analysis and Task Design.该系统首先深入探索存储库结构，了解环境、任务和评估的工作原理。然后，它研究代理的思维链日志和目标软件的 VLM 验证分数，识别故障模式。根据这些弱点，它设计了一个候选任务，其中包含详细的描述、数据要求和设置计划。在设计过程中，根据所有八个质量准则对任务进行评估，特别注意任务是否确实困难（不是人为困难）以及环境是否能够真正支持所提出的任务。The system begins with a deep exploration of the repository structure, understanding how environments, tasks, and evaluation work. It then studies the agent’s chain-of-thought logs and VLM verification scores for the target software, identifying patterns of failure. Based on these weaknesses, it designs a candidate task with a detailed description, data requirements, and setup plan. The task is evaluated against all eight quality guidelines during design, with particular attention to whether the task is genuinely difficult (not artificially hard) and whether the environment can actually support the proposed task.

第二阶段：评估和完善。Stage 2: Evaluation and Refinement.如果提出了多个候选任务，则每个任务都会根据每个质量标准进行严格评分。该系统被特别指示对标准 4（非人为困难）和标准 5（长期）至关重要：仅链接相同操作的任务If multiple candidate tasks were proposed, each is rigorously scored against every quality criterion. The system is specifically instructed to be critical on Criterion 4 (not artificially hard) and Criterion 5 (long horizon): tasks that merely chain the same operationN时间被拒绝为人为困难，而只需几十个步骤即可解决的任务则被拒绝为时间不够长。最佳候选者将被细化为最终的任务描述和设置计划。times are rejected as artificially hard, while tasks solvable in a few dozen steps are rejected as insufficiently long-horizon. The best candidate is refined into one final task description and setup plan.

第三阶段：实施。Stage 3: Implementation.系统至少研究目标软件的 3-4 个现有任务，以了解确切的文件模式，然后实现所有必需的文件：The system studies at least 3–4 existing tasks for the target software to learn the exact file patterns, then implements all required files:

  • task.json：任务描述、元数据和评估配置。: task description, metadata, and evaluation configuration.

  • setup_task.sh：下载数据、清除过时的输出、记录时间戳并启动应用程序。: downloads data, clears stale outputs, records timestamps, and launches the application.

  • export_result.sh：将文件存在、大小、时间戳和内容收集到结构化 JSON 中。: collects file existence, sizes, timestamps, and content into a structured JSON.

  • verifier.py：读取导出的 JSON 并计算分数。: reads the exported JSON and computes a score.

关键的实施限制包括确保答案不会在任务描述中泄露、保持跨系统的确定性以及准确遵循既定的模式。Key implementation constraints include ensuring the answer is not leaked in the task description, maintaining determinism across systems, and following established patterns exactly.

第四阶段：现场测试。Stage 4: Live Testing.系统生成一个实时环境实例，并对 12 个检查点执行详尽的验证：(1) 软件在正确状态下运行（通过屏幕截图验证），(2) 数据文件下载且有效，(3) 输出目录存在且具有正确的权限，(4) 过时的输出已正确清除，(5) 记录任务开始时间戳，(6) 任务描述中引用的所有数据元素都存在于数据中，(7) 软件中可以进行所有必需的操作，(8) 导出操作有效，(9) 文件写入权限是正确，(10)The system spawns a live environment instance and performs exhaustive verification of 12 checkpoints: (1) software running in correct state (verified via screenshots), (2) data files downloaded and valid, (3) output directories exist with correct permissions, (4) stale outputs properly cleared, (5) task start timestamp recorded, (6) all data elements referenced in the task description exist in the data, (7) all required operations are possible in the software, (8) export operations work, (9) file writing permissions are correct, (10)export_result.sh生成有效的 JSON，(11) 网络访问在需要时有效，(12) 所有引用的 GUI 元素均可访问。每项检查都通过实际命令或屏幕截图进行验证——没有任何假设。发现的任何问题都会立即在任务文件中修复。produces valid JSON, (11) network access works if required, (12) all referenced GUI elements are accessible. Every check is verified via actual commands or screenshots—nothing is assumed. Any issues discovered are fixed immediately in the task files.

第五阶段：证据收集。Stage 5: Evidence Collection.第二次验证可确保实时测试期间不会遗漏任何内容。该系统直接从运行环境收集全面的证据——原始日志、数据文件、配置转储和屏幕截图，并将它们存储在结构化证据目录中，并带有记录每个工件的自述文件。该证据包支持未来的人工审查和审计。A second verification pass ensures nothing was missed during live testing. The system collects comprehensive evidence—raw logs, data files, configuration dumps, and screenshots—directly from the running environment and stores them in a structured evidence directory with a README documenting each artifact. This evidence package enables future human review and audit.

第 6 阶段：PI 审核。Stage 6: PI Audit.在最后阶段，系统充当首席研究员 (PI) 审核员，独立验证任务文件中的每个元数据声明。对于任务中的每个键值对In the final stage, the system acts as a principal investigator (PI) auditor, independently verifying every metadata claim in the task files. For each key-value pair in the task

50

元数据，系统交叉引用实际数据文件、实时测试的计算以及权威的网络资源。每个声明都被标记为“已验证”、“有矛盾”或“未经验证”。审计产生一个metadata, the system cross-references the actual data files, computations from live testing, and authoritative web sources. Each claim is marked as “verified,” “contradicted,” or “unverified.” The audit produces avalidated_pi.json文件包含特权信息摘要，其中包含所有经过验证的地面实况值（精确测量、预期范围、数据怪异），VLM 评估人员稍后使用该信息对代理轨迹进行评分。file containing a privileged information summary with all verified ground truth values (exact measurements, expected ranges, data quirks), which is later used by VLM evaluators to score agent trajectories.

完整的管道针对 200 个软件环境中的每一个环境自主运行，每个阶段有两个小时的超时时间。失败的阶段会触发调查而不是盲目重试，系统会根据环境类型调整其缓存策略（容器化环境的 Docker 检查点、基于 VM 的环境的 QEMU 快照、Android 无缓存）。The full pipeline runs autonomously for each of the 200 software environments, with a two-hour timeout per stage. Failed stages trigger investigation rather than blind retry, and the system adapts its caching strategy based on environment type (Docker checkpoints for containerized environments, QEMU snapshots for VM-based environments, no caching for Android).

F.3 轨迹分析示例：3D 切片器F.3 Trajectory Analysis Example: 3D Slicer

为了说明代理故障分析如何驱动任务设计，我们提供了 3D Slicer 医学成像环境中的一个简短示例。下面的分析是根据原始轨迹分析笔记复制的，省略了不太重要的部分。To illustrate how agent failure analysis drives task design, we present an abbreviated example from the 3D Slicer medical imaging environment. The analysis below is reproduced from the original trajectory analysis notes, with less essential sections elided.

执行摘要。Executive Summary.对 Slicer3D 任务的 34 个模型轨迹的分析揭示了一个清晰的模式：Analysis of 34 model trajectories on Slicer3D tasks revealed a clear pattern:所有任务失败all tasks failed（通过率 0%），只有 2 项任务得分：(0% pass rate), with only 2 tasks scoring any points:

  • 肿瘤心室邻近度tumor_ventricle_proximity：31/100 分（最佳表现者）: 31/100 points (best performer)

  • ivc_直径_评估ivc_diameter_assessment：5/100分: 5/100 points

  • 所有其他 32 项任务All other 32 tasks: 0 分: 0 points

主要的失败模式是“滚动循环”——模型会无休止地滚动图像切片以寻找“完美”的解剖水平，而从未承诺实际进行测量。The primary failure mode is the “scrolling loop”—the model gets stuck endlessly scrolling through image slices looking for the “perfect” anatomical level, never committing to actually placing measurements.

失败模式：滚动循环。Failure Pattern: The Scrolling Loop.观察于Observed inaorta_measurement , ivc_diameter_assessment , cardiothoracic_ratio，以及大多数 0 分任务。模型正确识别任务（例如，“测量主动脉直径”），导航到标记模块，激活直线工具，然后开始滚动到“找到正确的解剖水平”——在整个图像体积中来回滚动，从不承诺进行测量，直到完成所有步骤。, and most 0-score tasks. The model correctly identifies the task (e.g., “measure aorta diameter”), navigates to the Markups module, activates the Line tool, then begins scrolling to “find the correct anatomical level”—scrolling back and forth through the entire image volume, never committing to placing a measurement, until all steps are consumed.

aorta_measurement（20 个步骤）：步骤 1-4 导航至“标记”并激活“直线”工具。步骤 5-20：滚动、滚动、滚动。 。 。 “继续滚动查找最大主动脉直径水平。”结果：0 分——未进行测量。(20 steps): Steps 1–4 navigate to Markups and activate the Line tool. Steps 5–20: scroll, scroll, scroll. . . “Continue scrolling to find the maximum aortic diameter level.” Result: 0 points—no measurement placed.

成功模式：果断行动。Success Pattern: Decisive Action.得分最高的任务（The highest-scoring task (tumor_ventricle_proximity, 31 分) 的不同之处在于数据是预先加载的并且相关结构可见。模型做到了, 31 points) differed in that data was pre-loaded with relevant structures visible. The model did不not广泛滚动 - 它立即在可见切片上放置测量值，然后成功导航到文件scroll extensively—it immediately placed a measurement on the visible slice, then successfully navigated File>保存对话框。在完成报告文件之前它已经完成了所有步骤，但核心测量是通过 6 个步骤完成的。Save dialogs. It ran out of steps before completing a report file, but the core measurement was done in 6 steps.

  • [. . . ]

已识别的代理弱点。Identified Agent Weaknesses.

  • 寻找特定的解剖水平Finding specific anatomical levels：模型缺乏领域知识来自信地识别最大主动脉直径水平、肝内与肾下 IVC、PA 分叉水平或椎体水平（L2、L3 等）。: the model lacks domain knowledge to confidently identify maximum aortic diameter level, intrahepatic vs. infrarenal IVC, PA bifurcation level, or vertebral levels (L2, L3, etc.).

  • 致力于在不确定的情况下采取行动Committing to action under uncertainty：当不确定切片是否“最佳”时，模型会继续无限期地搜索，永远不会做出“足够好”的决定。: when unsure if a slice is “optimal,” the model continues searching indefinitely, never making a “good enough” decision.

  • 复杂的多步骤医疗方案Complex multi-step medical protocols：需要不同级别的多次测量、特定解剖标志识别或临床解释的任务。: tasks requiring multiple measurements at different levels, specific anatomical landmark identification, or clinical interpretation.

这些故障模式直接影响了系统的设计These failure patterns directly inform the design of theCUA-World- 3D Slicer 的长期任务：生成的任务利用了智能体在不确定性下无法做出承诺的能力，同时需要跨多个解剖结构进行长期规划——这是一项真正困难的任务，而不仅仅是重复性任务。-Long task for 3D Slicer: the generated task exploits the agent’s inability to commit under uncertainty while requiring long-horizon planning across multiple anatomical structures—a genuinely difficult task, not merely a repetitive one.

H 污染物过滤详情H Contamination Filtering Details

拥有跨越 200 多个软件环境的 10,000 多个任务，With over 10,000 tasks spanning 200+ software environments,CUA-World必须确保训练集和测试集不包含接近重复或高度重叠的任务，这些任务的相似性会增加must ensure that train and test sets do not contain near-duplicate or highly overlapping tasks whose similarity would inflate

55

评价分数。我们实现了一个自动污染过滤管道，该管道（1）通过 LLM 判断对所有成对任务相似性进行评分，（2）从高相似性对构建污染图，（3）识别连接的组件，以及（4）将整个组件分配给相同的分割。evaluation scores. We implement an automated contamination filtering pipeline that (1) scores all pairwise task similarities via an LLM judge, (2) constructs a contamination graph from high-similarity pairs, (3) identifies connected components, and (4) assigns entire components to the same split.

H.1 成对相似度评分H.1 Pairwise Similarity Scoring

对于同一软件环境中的每对任务，我们提示法学硕士按照 8 点序数尺度对它们的相似性进行分类。表 12 定义了每个级别。For each pair of tasks within the same software environment, we prompt an LLM to classify their similarity on an 8-point ordinal scale. Table 12 defines each level.

表 12：LLM 法官使用的相似性量表。分数Table 12: Similarity scale used by the LLM judge. Scores≥4 被标记为潜在污染。4 are flagged as potentially contaminating.

分数Score 标签Label 描述Description
1 不相似NOTSIMILAR 完全不相关的任务（不同的目标，不同的领域）。Completely unrelated tasks (different goals, different domains).
2 有点相似SOMEWHATSIMILAR 主题略有重叠，但任务根本不同。Minor thematic overlap but fundamentally different tasks.
3 有些相似SOMESTEPSSIMILAR 任务共享一些共同的子步骤（例如，都导航到某个位置）Tasks share some common substeps (e.g., both navigate to a loca-
化），但最终目标截然不同。这是常见的并且tion) but have distinctly different end goals. This is common and
可以接受。acceptable.
4 非常相似VERYSIMILAR 任务极其相似——知道如何做一项任务会有所不同Tasks are extremely similar—knowing how to do one would di-
直接帮助对方。几乎可以互换。rectly help with the other. Nearly interchangeable.
5 同义SAMEREPHRASED 本质上是相同的任务，但措辞不同。Essentially the same task with different wording.
6 重复DUPLICATE 相同或接近相同的任务。Identical or near-identical tasks.
7 子集SUBSET 任务 1 是任务 2 的严格子集（任务 2 = 任务 1 + 附加任务Task 1 is a strict subset of Task 2 (Task 2 = Task 1 + additional
朝着同一个目标迈进）。steps toward the same goal).
8 超级组SUPERSET 任务 1 是任务 2 的严格超集（任务 1 = 任务 2 + 附加任务Task 1 is a strict superset of Task 2 (Task 1 = Task 2 + additional
朝着同一个目标迈进）。steps toward the same goal).

提示指示法官The prompt instructs the judge to be自由主义liberal：只有当任务真正可以互换或者一个任务严格包含另一个任务时，才应分配类别 4-8。共享公共子步骤（例如，“搜索位置”或“截取屏幕截图”）明确针对类别 3。完整的比较提示复制在附录 C.5 中。: categories 4–8 should only be assigned when tasks are truly interchangeable or one strictly contains the other. Sharing common substeps (e.g., “search for a location” or “take a screenshot”) is explicitly directed to category 3. The full comparison prompt is reproduced in Appendix C.5.

H.2 图构造和分割分配H.2 Graph Construction and Split Assignment

污染图。Contamination graph.我们设定的污染阈值是We set a contamination threshold ofτ= 4. 任何任务对 (= 4. Any task pair (钛、钛ti, tj) 接收相似度分数) receiving a similarity score≥ τ生成一个无向边generates an undirected edge in a污染图Gcontamination graph G，其中每个节点都是一个任务。, where each node is a task.

连接的组件。Connected components.我们计算的连通分量We compute the connected components ofG通过广度优先搜索。通过构造，组件中的每一对任务都通过至少一条污染相似性链传递链接。将组件中的所有任务分配给同一分割可保证训练/测试边界上的零污染对。via breadth-first search. By construction, every pair of tasks within a component is transitively linked through at least one chain of contaminating similarities. Assigning all tasks in a component to the same split guarantees zero contaminating pairs across the train/test boundary.

贪婪的装箱。Greedy bin-packing.组件按大小排序（最大的在前），并贪婪地分配给距其目标比率最远的分割（训练或测试）。分配后，自动验证通过确认没有污染边缘跨越分割。Components are sorted by size (largest first) and greedily assigned to whichever split—train or test—is furthest from its target ratio. After assignment, an automated verification pass confirms that no contaminating edge spans the split.

H.3 汇总统计H.3 Aggregate Statistics

表 13 总结了过滤结果Table 13 summarizes the filtering outcome acrossCUA-World .

绝大多数任务对 (98.2%) 的得分为 3（某些步骤相似），反映了同一软件中的任务共享导航子步骤但追求不同目标的预期模式。只有 0.65% 的比较被标记为污染。在标记对中，得分 4（非常相似）是最常见的，而真正的重复项（得分 6）相对较少，这表明任务生成管道生成不同的任务，仅偶尔出现接近重叠的任务。The overwhelming majority of task pairs (98.2%) receive a score of 3 (SOME STEPS SIMILAR), reflecting the expected pattern that tasks within the same software share navigational substeps but pursue distinct goals. Only 0.65% of comparisons are flagged as contaminating. Among flagged pairs, score 4 (VERY SIMILAR) is the most common, while true duplicates (score 6) are comparatively rare, indicating that the task generation pipeline produces diverse tasks with only occasional near-overlaps.

连接组件之间 94.3% 的单例率证实了污染是稀疏的：大多数任务没有污染邻居，并且可以自由分配给任一分割。其余组件将相关任务的小集群组合在一起，并保存在一个拆分中。The 94.3% singleton rate among connected components confirms that contamination is sparse: most tasks have no contaminating neighbor and can be freely assigned to either split. The remaining components group small clusters of related tasks that are kept together in a single split.

H.4 手动验证H.4 Manual Verification

除了自动化管道之外，我们还提供了一个基于网络的验证仪表板，它并排显示标记的任务对，以及它们的相似性分数和法学硕士法官的推理。注释者可以审查边界情况（特别是那些处于分数 4 阈值边界的情况），并在必要时覆盖自动判断。这个人机交互步骤是确保训练/测试分离完整性的最终保障。In addition to the automated pipeline, we provide a web-based verification dashboard that displays flagged task pairs side by side, along with their similarity scores and the LLM judge’s reasoning. Annotators can review borderline cases (particularly those at the threshold boundary of score 4) and override the automated judgment if necessary. This human-in-the-loop step serves as a final safeguard to ensure the integrity of the train/test split.

完整的比较提示转载于附录 C.5。The full comparison prompt is reproduced in Appendix C.5.

一、软件分类I Software Categorization

我们沿着两个轴对 200 多个软件产品中的每一个进行分类，以分析软件属性如何影响代理性能（§5）。We classify each of the 200+ software products along two axes to analyze how software properties affect agent performance (§5).

视觉复杂性。Visual complexity.我们将每个软件的界面评为We rate each software’s interface as低low , 中等medium, 或, or高high基于其主要工作空间的视觉密度和空间复杂性：based on the visual density and spatial complexity of its primary workspace:

  • 低Low（83 软件）：扁平化 UI，具有标准 Web 表单、文本布局、清晰的标签和按钮。示例：WordPress、LibreOffice Writer、Chrome、ERPNext、OpenEMR。(83 software): Flat UI with standard web forms, text-heavy layouts, clear labels and buttons. Examples: WordPress, LibreOffice Writer, Chrome, ERPNext, OpenEMR.

  • 中等Medium（91 软件）：具有选项卡式界面、数据表、图表和中等视觉密度的多面板布局。示例：VS Code、DBeaver、Wireshark、LibreOffice Calc、Jenkins。(91 software): Multi-panel layouts with tabbed interfaces, data tables, charts, and moderate visual density. Examples: VS Code, DBeaver, Wireshark, LibreOffice Calc, Jenkins.

  • 高High（34 个软件）：空间画布、3D 视口、密集科学可视化、地图图层、波形显示或多面板成像。示例：Blender、GIMP、QGIS、3D Slicer、AstroImageJ、Ardour。(34 software): Spatial canvases, 3D viewports, dense scientific visualizations, map layers, waveform displays, or multi-panel imaging. Examples: Blender, GIMP, QGIS, 3D Slicer, AstroImageJ, Ardour.

领域知识。Domain knowledge.我们将软件是否需要特定领域的专业知识分类为We classify whether the software requires domain-specific expertise as一般general or 专门的specialized :

  • 一般General（104 软件）：懂计算机的人可以通过阅读 UI 来完成任务。不需要特定领域的培训。示例：网络浏览器、电子邮件客户端、CRM/ERP 系统、办公套件、项目管理工具。(104 software): A computer-literate person can accomplish tasks by reading the UI. No field-specific training is needed. Examples: web browsers, email clients, CRM/ERP systems, office suites, project management tools.

  • 专业化Specialized（104 软件）：需要了解特定的专业或科学领域（其概念、术语或方法）才能知道要做什么。示例：3D Slicer（医学成像）、AstroImageJ（天文光度测量）、HEC-RAS（水力建模）、PyMOL（分子可视化）、Wireshark（网络协议）。(104 software): Requires understanding a specific professional or scientific domain— its concepts, terminology, or methods—to know what to do. Examples: 3D Slicer (medical imaging), AstroImageJ (astrophotometry), HEC-RAS (hydraulic modeling), PyMOL (molecular visualization), Wireshark (network protocols).

57

分类基于软件的界面和领域，而不是为其生成的特定任务。补充代码中提供了所有软件对两个轴的完整分配（The categorization is based on the software’s interface and domain, not the specific tasks generated for it. The full assignment of all software to both axes is available in the supplementary code (analysis/categorize_envs.py ).

K.2 评估模型K.2 Evaluated Models

我们评估了四个前沿模型We evaluate four frontier models onCUA-World-长：Gemini 3 Flash、Kimi-K 2.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 GPT-5.4。我们不评估 Claude Opus 4.6-Long: Gemini 3 Flash, Kimi-K 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and GPT-5.4. We do not evaluate Claude Opus 4.6 onCUA-World- 由于成本限制，时间较长。此外，200 个任务中每个任务的预算为 5 美元，这意味着 opus 将使用更少的步骤（-Long due to cost constraints. Furhter, $5-per-task budget across 200 tasks, would imply opus would use much fewer steps (∼100）比其他型号。此外，Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 在 OSWorld 上实现了几乎相同的性能（72.7 与 72.5），这表明额外的成本可能会在我们的基准测试中产生有限的额外信号。100) than other models. Furthermore, Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6 achieve nearly identical performance on OSWorld (72.7 vs. 72.5), suggesting that the additional cost would likely yield limited additional signal on our benchmark.

代理线束。Agent harnesses.对于 GPT-5.4 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6，我们使用其各自文档中的官方代理工具。 [23]对于 Gemini 3 Flash 和 Kimi-K 2.5，在我们进行实验时官方工具尚未公开，因此我们从 OSWorld 存储库改编了 Qwen3-VL 工具。 [4]对于 Qwen3-VL 学生模型，我们直接使用 OSWorld 线束。For GPT-5.4 and Claude Sonnet 4.6, we use their official agent harnesses from their respective documentation.[23] For Gemini 3 Flash and Kimi-K 2.5, official harnesses were not publicly available at the time of our experiments, so we adapted the Qwen3-VL harness from the OSWorld repository.[4] For the Qwen3-VL student model, we use the OSWorld harness directly.

L 提议并放大消融：定性分析L Propose-and-Amplify Ablation: Qualitative Analysis

为了理解为什么种子任务可以提高放大任务的质量（§4），我们比较了在三个代表性软件应用程序上使用和不使用种子示例生成的任务：Firefox（网络浏览器）、AstroImageJ（天文图像分析）和 Moodle（学习管理系统）。我们从三个维度分析差异。To understand why seed tasks improve amplified task quality (§4), we compare tasks generated with and without seed examples on three representative software applications: Firefox (web browser), AstroImageJ (astronomical image analysis), and Moodle (learning management system). We analyze the differences along three dimensions.

现实主义。Realism.对于种子，任务以专业工作流程为基础：Firefox 任务涉及从特定角色（例如调查记者或开发总监）的角度研究真实网站（Grants.gov、NSF Award Search）； AstroImageJ 任务参考真实的天文物体（鹰星云、WASP-12b）和真实技术（光子传输 CCD 增益测量、光曲线去趋势）； Moodle 任务反映了实际的机构运营（等级审核、具有特定功能的自定义角色创建）。如果没有种子，任务就会转向功能演示：Firefox 任务专注于浏览器功能（导入 CA 证书、导出 TLS 证书），AstroImageJ 任务变成通用处理操作（对齐图像、创建主暗框），并且很大程度上不存在特定于领域的专业上下文。 Moodle 是一个部分例外，可能是因为它的结构在 LLM 培训数据中得到了很好的体现。With seeds, tasks are grounded in professional workflows: Firefox tasks involve researching real websites (Grants.gov, NSF Award Search) from the perspective of specific roles such as an investigative journalist or a development director; AstroImageJ tasks reference real astronomical objects (Eagle Nebula, WASP-12b) and real techniques (photon transfer CCD gain measurement, light curve detrending); Moodle tasks reflect actual institutional operations (grade auditing, custom role creation with specific capabilities). Without seeds, tasks shift toward feature demonstrations: Firefox tasks focus on browser features (importing a CA certificate, exporting a TLS certificate), AstroImageJ tasks become generic processing operations (align images, create a master dark frame), and the domain-specific professional context is largely absent. Moodle is a partial exception, likely because its structure is well-represented in LLM training data.

65

难度和视野。Difficulty and horizon.对于种子，任务需要 50-80 个步骤以及链接多个操作的多阶段工作流程：研究、下载、组织和综合 (Firefox)；加载数据、处理、测量和报告（AstroImageJ）；配置数据库、设置权限并通过日志进行验证 (Moodle)。如果没有种子，任务通常需要 30-50 个步骤，并且涉及单特征操作。例如，Firefox TLS 证书导出任务减少为访问网页、单击挂锁和下载证书。With seeds, tasks require 50–80 steps with multi-stage workflows that chain several operations: research, download, organize, and synthesize (Firefox); load data, process, measure, and report (AstroImageJ); configure database, set permissions, and verify via logs (Moodle). Without seeds, tasks typically require 30–50 steps and involve single-feature operations. For instance, a Firefox TLS certificate export task reduces to visiting a webpage, clicking the padlock, and downloading the certificate.

设置脚本质量。Setup script quality.使用种子，设置脚本的范围从 50 到 164 行，并执行大量数据准备：从哈勃档案下载真实的天文数据，使用物理上合理的噪声模型（泊松噪声、读取噪声、暗电流）生成合成 FITS 文件，并使用多表 SQL 插入填充数据库。如果没有种子，安装脚本有 40-120 行，通常只需启动应用程序并打开 URL，只需最少的数据准备。这使得任务更加脆弱（依赖于外部网络资源）并且可重复性较差。一个例外是 Firefox 自定义 CA 导入任务（无种子），它在 140 行中创建了完整的 PKI 基础设施，表明该模型偶尔可以在没有种子的情况下生成高质量的设置，但效果不一致。With seeds, setup scripts range from 50 to 164 lines and perform substantial data preparation: downloading real astronomical data from Hubble archives, generating synthetic FITS files with physically plausible noise models (Poisson noise, read noise, dark current), and populating databases with multi-table SQL inserts. Without seeds, setup scripts are 40–120 lines and often just launch the application and open a URL, with minimal data preparation. This makes tasks more fragile (dependent on external network resources) and less reproducible. One exception is a Firefox custom CA import task (without seeds), which creates a full PKI infrastructure in 140 lines, showing that the model can occasionally produce high-quality setups without seeds, but does so inconsistently.

总结。Summary.种子任务教会放大器仅靠提示无法完成的两件事：（1）特定软件的实际专业工作是什么样子，以及（2）如何使用真实或现实的数据准备丰富的初始状态。如果没有这些示例，模型就会退回到软件功能的一般知识，从而生成更简单、不太现实且可重复性较差的任务。The seed tasks teach the amplifier two things the prompt alone cannot: (1) what realistic professional work looks like for a specific software, and (2) how to prepare a rich initial state with real or realistic data. Without these examples, the model falls back to its generic knowledge of the software’s features, producing tasks that are simpler, less realistic, and less reproducible.

M轨迹行为分析M Trajectory Behavioral Analysis

了解代理的行为方式To understand how agents behave onCUA-World，我们在评估运行中运行了超过 2,981 个轨迹（701 个通过，2,280 个失败）的自动行为分析管道。该管道分三个阶段运行。, we run an automated behavioral analysis pipeline over 2,981 trajectories (701 passed, 2,280 failed) from our evaluation runs. The pipeline operates in three stages.

第一阶段：每个轨迹的行为总结。Stage 1: Per-trajectory behavioral summary.每个轨迹（屏幕截图、操作和模型响应的完整序列）都会输入 LLM，后者将代理的行为分解为自然阶段并生成高级行为摘要。至关重要的是，法学硕士不会被告知轨迹是通过还是失败，也没有被给予受控词汇或预定义类别——它用自己的语言描述了发生的事情。这避免了分析偏向于预期的故障模式。Each trajectory (the full sequence of screenshots, actions, and model responses) is fed to an LLM, which decomposes the agent’s behavior into natural phases and produces a high-level behavioral summary. Crucially, the LLM is not told whether the trajectory passed or failed, and is given no controlled vocabulary or predefined categories—it describes what happened in its own words. This avoids biasing the analysis toward expected failure modes.

第二阶段：模式发现。Stage 2: Pattern discovery.每个轨迹的摘要被打乱到随机混合环境批次中，并输入到 LLM，后者识别每个批次中重复出现的行为模式。该指令需要与环境无关的语言：没有软件名称、没有 UI 元素名称、没有特定于应用程序的术语。处理完所有批次后，合并过程会将重叠模式合并为一组规范的 15 个重复数据删除模式。每个模式都是对重复行为的简短描述（例如，“当操作未生效时，代理进入重试循环”）。The per-trajectory summaries are shuffled into random mixedenvironment batches and fed to an LLM, which identifies recurring behavioral patterns across each batch. The instruction requires environment-agnostic language: no software names, no UI element names, no application-specific terminology. After all batches are processed, a consolidation pass merges overlapping patterns into a canonical set of 15 deduplicated patterns. Each pattern is a short description of a recurring behavior (e.g., “the agent enters retry loops when actions do not take effect”).

第三阶段：模式匹配。Stage 3: Pattern matching.对于每条轨迹，我们将其行为摘要与 15 种规范模式一起发送给法学硕士，并询问存在哪些模式。轨迹中的每个步骤都可以用多个模式标记（例如，一个步骤可能涉及 UI 探索和重试），因此模式之间的分数之和不等于 1。对于每个模式，我们计算两个指标：For each trajectory, we send its behavioral summary alongside the 15 canonical patterns to an LLM and ask which patterns are present. Each step in a trajectory can be tagged with multiple patterns (e.g., a step may involve both UI exploration and a retry), so fractions across patterns do not sum to one. For each pattern, we compute two metrics:阶跃分数step fraction（轨迹步数的多少部分表现出该模式，跨轨迹平均）以及(what fraction of a trajectory’s steps exhibit the pattern, averaged across trajectories) and存在率presence rate（轨迹的多少部分至少展示一次该模式）。这两个指标都是针对通过和失败的轨迹单独计算的。(what fraction of trajectories exhibit the pattern at least once). Both metrics are computed separately for passed and failed trajectories.

结果。Results.图 11 显示了所有 15 种模式的阶梯分数视图，图 12 显示了存在率视图。主论文（§5）中强调的三种模式是Figure 11 shows the step-fraction view and Figure 12 shows the presence-rate view across all 15 patterns. The three patterns highlighted in the main paper (§5) are重试循环retry loops , 用户界面探索UI exploration, 和, and验证检查verification checks。除此之外，其他一些模式显示了通过和失败的轨迹之间的显着差距。例如，. Beyond these, several additional patterns show notable gaps between passed and failed trajectories. For instance,访问拦截器access blockers（身份验证失败、无法访问的服务）在失败的轨迹中占据 23% 的步骤，但在通过的轨迹中只占 4%。(authentication failures, unreachable services) occupy 23% of steps in failed trajectories but only 4% in passed ones.工具旋转Tool pivoting38% 的失败轨迹中存在代理放弃主要 GUI 并切换到 CLI 或替代工具的情况，但只有 19% 的通过轨迹中出现这种情况。从积极的一面来看，—where the agent abandons the primary GUI and switches to CLI or alternative tools—is present in 38% of failed trajectories but only 19% of passed ones. On the positive side,保存/导出save/export52% 的通过轨迹中存在步骤，但只有 33% 的失败轨迹中存在步骤，这反映出成功的智能体通常会到达任务的最后阶段。steps are present in 52% of passed trajectories but only 33% of failed ones, reflecting that successful agents more often reach the final stage of the task.
